[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:2)


**בָּאִין מִתְּרוּמַת הַלִּשְׁכָּה.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּפָרָה אֵינָהּ נִשְׁחֶטֶת בָּעֲזָרָה, בָּאָה מִתְּרוּמַת הַלִּשְׁכָּה, דְּחַטָּאת קָרְיָהּ רַחֲמָנָא. וְשָׂעִיר הַמִּשְׁתַּלֵּחַ, לְפִי שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִקַּח שְׁנֵי שְׂעִירִים וְאֵין יָדוּעַ עַל אֵיזֶה מֵהֶם יַעֲלֶה הַגּוֹרָל לַה':


באין מתרומת הלשכה – come from the Temple fund/treasury chamber Even though the [red] heifer is not slaughtered in the Temple courtyard it comes from the Temple fund/treasury chamber, for the All-Merciful calls it a “sin-offering” (Numbers 19:9 – which is translated as “It is for cleansing.”). And the scapegoat – because it requires taking two goats and it is not known upon which of them will come up the lot “for God” (Leviticus 16:10).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:3)


**וְלָשׁוֹן שֶׁל זְהוֹרִית.** שְׁנִי תוֹלַעַת שֶׁמַּשְׁלִיכִין אֶל תּוֹךְ שְׂרֵפַת הַפָּרָה. וְהוּא הַדִּין לְעֵץ אֶרֶז וְאֵזוֹב, אֶלָּא נָקַט לָשׁוֹן שֶׁל זְהוֹרִית לְבַד לְחַלֵּק בֵּין לָשׁוֹן שֶׁל זְהוֹרִית זֶה לַלָּשׁוֹן שֶׁל זְהוֹרִית שֶׁל שָׂעִיר הַמִּשְׁתַּלֵּחַ:


ולשון של זהורית – the scarlet yarn/crimson thread that they cast into the burning of the heifer; and the same law applies to the cedar wood and hyssop, but it (i.e., the Mishnah) took the language of the crimson thread alone to distinguish between this crimson thread and the language of the thread upon the scapegoat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:4)


**כֶּבֶשׁ פָּרָה.** שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂים שְׁנֵי גְּשָׁרִים זֶה עַל גַּבֵּי זֶה מִפְּנֵי קֶבֶר הַתְּהוֹם מֵהַר הַבַּיִת לְהַר הַמִּשְׁחָה, וַעֲלֵיהֶן הָיוּ מוֹצִיאִין אֶת הַפָּרָה:


כבש פרה – they would make two bridges, one on top of the other because of the grave in the depth (i.e., a covered-up uncleanness discovered) from the Temple Mount to the Mount of Olives and upon them, they would remove the heifer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:5)


**וְכֶבֶשׁ [שָׂעִיר].** שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂין כְּמִין גֶּשֶׁר עַד חוּץ לָעִיר, וְעָלָיו הָיָה הוֹלֵךְ הַמְשַׁלֵּחַ אֶת הַשָּׂעִיר, מִפְּנֵי הַבַּבְלִיִּים שֶׁהָיוּ תּוֹלְשִׁים שְׂעָרוֹ וְאוֹמְרִים לוֹ טֹל וְצֵא שֶׁלֹּא תַּשְׁהֶה עֲוֹנוֹתֵינוּ:


וכבש [שעיר] – they would make a kind of bridge until the exit from the city, and upon it would go the scapegoat because of the Babylonians who would pluck out its hair and say: “take and leave so that our sins will not tarry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:6)


**וְלָשׁוֹן שֶׁבֵּין קַרְנָיו.** לֵידַע אִם הִלְבִּין וְנִתְכַּפְּרוּ עֲווֹנוֹתֵיהֶן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל. וְהָנֵי אֵינָם צֹרֶךְ קָרְבָּן, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינָן בָּאִין מִתְּרוּמָה שֶׁהֻפְרְשָׁה לְשֵׁם קָרְבָּנוֹת, אֶלָּא מִמַּה שֶּׁנִּשְׁתַּיֵּר בַּלִּשְׁכָּה אַחַר הַפְרָשַׁת הַתְּרוּמוֹת:


ולשון שבין קרניו – to know if it had turned white and the sins of the Jewish people had been atoned for, and these do not require a sacrifice. Therefore, they do not come from the Terumah/sacred contributions that were separated for the sake of sacrifices but rather, from what remained in the compartment after the separation of the Terumot/sacred contributions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:7)


**וְאַמַּת הַמַּיִם.** הָעוֹבֶרֶת בָּעֲזָרָה, אִם צְרִיכָה תִּקּוּן:


ואמת המים – that passes through the Temple courtyard if it needs repair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:8)


**וְכָל צָרְכֵי הָעִיר.** לַחְפֹּר בּוֹרוֹת שִׁיחִין וּמְעָרוֹת, וְתִקּוּן רְחוֹבוֹתֶיהָ וּשְׁוָקֶיהָ, וּשְׁמִירַת הָעִיר:


וכל צרכי העיר – to dig cisterns, pits and caves, and the repair of its streets, its marketplaces and the guarding of the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:2:9)


**אַבָּא שָׁאוּל אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּאַבָּא שָׁאוּל:


אבא שאול אומר כו'- but the Halakha is not according to Abba Sahul.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:3:1)


**מוֹתַר שְׁיָרֵי הַלִּשְׁכָּה.** מַה שֶּׁנִּשְׁאַר אַחַר שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה מֵהֶן תִּקּוּן צָרְכֵי הָעִיר:


מותר שירי הלשכה – what remained after the repairs for the needs of the city were made from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:3:2)


**לוֹקְחִים מֵהֶן יֵינוֹת שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת.** וּמוֹכְרִים אוֹתָן לְמִי שֶׁצָּרִיךְ יַיִן לִנְסָכִים וּשְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת לִמְנָחוֹת:


לוקחים מהן יינות שמנים וסלתות – and they sell it to those who need wine for libations and oils and fine flour for meal-offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:3:3)


**אֵין מִשְׂתַּכְּרִין בְּשֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ.** שֶׁאֵין עֲנִיּוּת בְּמָקוֹם עֲשִׁירוּת, וּגְנַאי הוּא לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ:


אין משתכרין משל הקדש – for the poor people are not in the place of the rich ones, and it is a disgrace to the Temple property.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:3:4)


**וְאַף לֹא בְּשֶׁל עֲנִיִּים.** דִּלְמָא מִתְרְמֵי עָנִי וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵיתָב לֵיהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


ואף לא בשל עניים – lest a poor person donate it and there is none to give him, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:1)


**מוֹתַר הַתְּרוּמָה.** הַיְנוּ מַה שֶּׁנִּשְׁאַר בַּקֻּפּוֹת בְּרֹאשׁ חֹדֶשׁ נִיסָן, שֶׁאָז מְבִיאִין קָרְבָּנוֹת מִתְּרוּמָה חֲדָשָׁה:


מותר תרומה – that is what remains in the boxes/baskets on Rosh Hodesh Nisan, for then we bring sacrifices from the new sacred donations.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:2)


**צִפּוּי לְבֵית קָדְשֵׁי הַקָּדָשִׁים.** לָרִצְפָּה וְלַכְּתָלִים:


צפוי לבית קדש הקדשים – for the floor and the walls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:3)


**מוֹתַר פֵּרוֹת.** רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל לְטַעֲמֵיהּ, דְּאָמַר לְעֵיל מוֹתַר שְׁיָרֵי הַלִּשְׁכָּה קוֹנִים מֵהֶן יֵינוֹת שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת, וְהַשָּׂכָר שֶׁמִּשְׂתַּכְּרִים בָּהֶן קָרוּי מוֹתַר פֵּרוֹת, כְּלוֹמַר מַה שֶּׁהוֹתִירוּ וְנִשְׂתַּכְּרוּ בַּפֵּרוֹת שֶׁקָּנוּ:


מותר פירות – Rabbi Yishmael, according to his reasoning, who said above that the surplus of the leftovers of the chamber, we purchase with them wines, oils and fine flour and the income that we make through them is called "מותר הפירות"/surplus of the gains of the produce – that is to say, what remained and was earned from the produce that they bought.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:4)


**קַיִץ לַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** כְּשֶׁהַמִּזְבֵּחַ בָּטֵל מְבִיאִים מֵהֶן עוֹלוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לְהַעֲלוֹת מִינֵי מְתִיקָה עַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן בִּגְמַר סְעוּדָתָן, כָּךְ אַחַר שֶׁגָּמְרוּ לְהָבִיא חוֹבַת הַיּוֹם מְבִיאִים עוֹלוֹת הַלָּלוּ כְּשֶׁאֵין נְדָרִים וּנְדָבוֹת וְהַמִּזְבֵּחַ בָּטֵל:


קיץ למזבח – when the altar is idle, we bring from them burnt offerings like the manner of human beings to bring up all kinds of sweets on to the table at the conclusion of the meal, so to, after they have completed bringing the obligatory [offerings] of the day, they bring these burnt offerings, when there are no votive or freewill offerings and the altar is idle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:5)


**מוֹתַר תְּרוּמָה לִכְלֵי שָׁרֵת.** מִקְרָא דָּרֵישׁ לֵיהּ דִּכְתִיב (דברי הימים ב כד) וְאֶת שְׁאָר הַכֶּסֶף וַיַּעֲשֵׂהוּ כֵלִים לְבֵית ה', אֵיזֶהוּ כֶּסֶף שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שִׁירַיִם, הֲוֵי אוֹמֵר זוֹ תְּרוּמַת הַלִּשְׁכָּה:


מותר תרומה לכלי שרת (see Chapter 3, Mishnah 2 of Tractate Shekalim) – a Biblical verse is expounded regarding this (II Chronicles 24:14): “[When they had finished, they brought] the money that was left over [to the king and Jehoiada (the High Priest); it was made into utensils for the House of the LORD,” What is the money that has leftovers? I would state that this is the sacred donations to the chamber.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:6)


**מוֹתַר תְּרוּמָה לְקַיִץ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** שֶׁלְּצֹרֶךְ קָרְבָּנוֹת הֻפְרְשָׁה:


מותר תרומה לקיץ המזבח – for the needs of the sacred donations that were separated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:7)


**מוֹתַר נְסָכִים.** גִּזְבְּרֵי הֶקְדֵּשׁ הָיוּ מַמְצִיאִים מָעוֹת לְבַעֲלֵי יֵינוֹת וּשְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת, לְסַפֵּק לִמְנָחוֹת וּנְסָכִים כָּל הַשָּׁנָה, וְאִם קִבֵּל עָלָיו לְסַפֵּק שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִים בְּסֶלַע וְעָמְדוּ אַרְבַּע סְאִים בְּסֶלַע, צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתֵּן אַרְבַּע סְאִין, וְאוֹתָהּ סְאָה הִיא מוֹתַר נְסָכִים. וְכֵן כְּשֶׁמּוֹדְדִין לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ הָיוּ מוֹדְדִין בְּמִדָּה מְבֹרֶצֶת וּגְדוּשָׁה, וְהַגִּזְבָּר הָיָה מוֹחֵק הַמִּדָּה, הַבֵּרוּץ נִקְרָא מוֹתַר נְסָכִים, וְעוֹשִׂין מֵהֶן כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת, שֶׁהַנְּסָכִים מִתְקַדְּשִׁים בִּכְלֵי שָׁרֵת:


מותר נסכים – the treasurers of the Temple treasury would furnish money to the owners of the wine, oils and fine flour to provide for the meal-offerings and libations all year. But if he (i.e., the owner) accepted upon himself to provide three Se’ah for a Sela and it (i.e., the going market price) stood at four Se’ah for a Sela, it was necessary for him to give four Se’ah and that Se’ah was the surplus from the libations. For just as they measure for the Temple treasury, so would measure that was cut through from end to end and overflowing. But the treasurer would erase the measure that was cut through from end to end which is called the surplus from the libations and make of them vessels of the ministry that the libations would be sanctified in vessels of the ministry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:8)


**מוֹתַר נְסָכִים לְקַיִץ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** שֶׁהַנְּסָכִים כָּלִיל וְהָעוֹלָה כָּלִיל:


מותר נסכים לקיץ המזבח – when the libations ran out and the burnt offerings ran out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:9)


**זֶה וָזֶה.** רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וְרַבִּי חֲנִינָא:


זה וזה – Rabbi Akiba and Rabbi Hanina.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:4:10)


**לֹא הָיוּ מוֹדִים בַּפֵּרוֹת.** כִּדְאָמַר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא לְעֵיל, שֶׁאֵין מִשְׂתַּכְּרִים בְּשֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ. וּמַסְקָנָא דִּתְנַאי בֵּית דִּין הוּא עַל הַמּוֹתָרוֹת שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כֻּלָּן קְרֵבִים עוֹלוֹת. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


לא היו מודים בפירות – just as Rabbi Akiba said above (Mishnah three of this chapter): They may not make profit from sacred property, and the conclusion is that it is a condition of the Jewish court regarding the surpluses that all of them should be offered as burnt offerings, and this is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:1)


**מוֹתַר הַקְּטֹרֶת.** הַנּוֹתָר בְּכָל שָׁנָה:


נותר הקטורת – that remains every year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:2)


**מֶה הָיוּ עוֹשִׂין בָּהּ.** לְהַקְטִירָהּ לַשָּׁנָה הַבָּאָה. שֶׁאֵין לְךָ שָׁנָה שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ מוֹתָר, שֶׁהַקְּטֹרֶת הָיְתָה נַעֲשֵׂית שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת שִׁשִּׁים וּשְׁמֹנָה מָנִים, שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת שִׁשִּׁים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מָנִים כְּמִנְיָן שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת שִׁשִּׁים וַחֲמִשָּׁה יְמוֹת הַחַמָּה, וּשְׁלֹשָׁה מָנִין שֶׁמֵּהֶם כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל מַכְנִיס מְלֹא חָפְנָיו בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים, וְלֹא הָיוּ נִכְנָסִים כֻּלָּן בְּחָפְנָיו. וְעוֹד, בְּכָל שָׁנָה פְּשׁוּטָה הָיָה מוֹתָר, שֶׁשָּׁנָה פְּשׁוּטָה יָמֶיהָ שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת חֲמִשִּׁים וְאַרְבָּעָה:


מה היו עושין בה – to offer as incense to God in the next year, for there is no year without surplus for the incense was made into three-hundred and sixty-eight minas for three-hundred and sixty-five minas is like the three hundred and sixty-five days of the solar [year] and three minas that from them, the High Priest would bring in his handfuls on Yom Kippur but they would not all enter with their handfuls. And furthermore, in each ordinary year, there was a surplus since an ordinary year had three-hundred and fifty-four days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:3)


**מַפְרִישִׁין מִמֶּנָּה.** מִן הַלִּשְׁכָּה:


מפרישין ממנה – from the chamber.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:4)


**שְׂכַר הָאֻמָּנִין.** מִפַּטָּמֵי הַקְּטֹרֶת, שֶׁהֵן נוֹטְלִים שְׂכָרָן מִתְּרוּמַת הַלִּשְׁכָּה. וּמְזַכִּין הַמָּעוֹת לָאֻמָּנִין עַל יַד אֶחָד מִן הַגִּזְבָּרִים, וַהֲרֵי הֵן חֻלִּין. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין הֶקְדֵּשׁ יוֹצֵא לְחֻלִּין אִם לֹא שֶׁיִּכָּנֵס דָּבָר אַחֵר תַּחְתָּיו לִקְדֻשָּׁה, תְּרוּמַת הַלִּשְׁכָּה שָׁאנֵי, דְּבֵית דִּין מַתְנֶה עֲלֵיהֶם לִתְּנָם לְכַמָּה דְּבָרִים. וְאַחֲרֵי שֶׁזָּכוּ בַּמָּעוֹת לְצֹרֶךְ הָאֻמָּנִין מְחַלְּלִין הַקְּטֹרֶת עַל אוֹתָן הַמָּעוֹת, וְהַמָּעוֹת קְדוֹשִׁים לִתְרוּמָה יְשָׁנָה, וְנוֹתְנִים הַקְּטֹרֶת לָאֻמָּנִים בִּשְׂכָרָם, וְחוֹזְרִין וְקוֹנִין אוֹתָהּ מֵהֶן בְּמָעוֹת שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה חֲדָשָׁה. וְהָכִי צְנִיעָא מִלְּתָא טְפֵי, וְלֹא לְמָכְרָהּ וְלַחֲזֹר וְלִקְנוֹתָהּ:


שכר האומנין – from the pounding of the incense, they would take their payment from the chamber of the sacred donations and they would take possession of the monies for artisans through one hand from the treasurers and they are non-holy [produce], and even though what is holy does not become non-holy if they don’t bring something else in its place for holiness. The sacred donations of the chamber are different since the Jewish court makes a condition upon them to give them for several things. But after the monies were transferred for the needs of the artisans, they would redeem the incense with those monies. But the sacred monies from the old sacred donations, they would give the incense to the artisans as their payment and go back and repurchase it from them with monies of the new sacred donations. And in this way, the matter was much more discreet to not sell it and then go back and repurchase it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:5)


**אִם בָּא הֶחָדָשׁ בִּזְמַנּוֹ.** שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ שְׁקָלִים חֲדָשִׁים קֹדֶם רֹאשׁ חֹדֶשׁ נִיסָן, שֶׁהוּא זְמַן הַתְּרוּמָה הַחֲדָשָׁה, לוֹקְחִים מוֹתַר הַקְּטֹרֶת מִתְּרוּמָה חֲדָשָׁה עַל יְדֵי חִלּוּל כִּדְפָרְשִׁינַן לְעֵיל:


אם בא חודש בזמנו – that they brought new Shekalim prior to Rosh Hodesh Nisan which is the time for the new sacred donations, they purchase the surplus of the incense from the new sacred donations through redemption as we explained above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:5:6)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיאוּ שְׁקָלִים חֲדָשִׁים עֲדַיִן, לוֹקְחִים אוֹתָהּ מִן הַתְּרוּמָה יְשָׁנָה, אִם חִלְּלוּהָ כְּבָר. וְאִם לֹא חִלְּלוּהָ, מַקְטִירִין אוֹתָהּ, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁלֹּא בָּא הֶחָדָשׁ צָרִיךְ לְהָבִיא מִן הַיְּשָׁנָה:


ואם לאו – they had not yet brought their new Shekalim, they purchase it from the old sacred donations if they redeemed it already, and if they had not redeemed it, they offer incense for since the new [donations] had not yet come, they must bring it from the old.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:6:1)


**הַמַּקְדִּישׁ נְכָסָיו.** וּסְתָם הֶקְדֵּשׁ לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת:


המקדיש נכסיו – and an undefined dedication to Temple property is for the repair and upkeep of the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:6:2)


**וְהָיוּ בָהֶם דְּבָרִים הָרְאוּיִם לְקָרְבְּנוֹת צִבּוּר.** כְּגוֹן קְטֹרֶת, אוֹ יֵינוֹת, שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת:


והיו בהם דברים הראויים לקרבנות צבור – such as incense or wines, oils and fine flour.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:6:3)


**יִנָּתְנוּ לָאֻמָּנִין בִּשְׂכָרָן.** וְיוֹצְאִין לְחֻלִּין, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין דָּבָר אַחֵר נִכְנַס תַּחְתָּיו. דְּקָסָבַר הֶקְדֵּשׁ מִתְחַלֵּל עַל הַמְּלָאכָה, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כה) וְעָשׂוּ לִי מִקְדָּשׁ, שֶׁתִּהְיֶה הַמְּלָאכָה נַעֲשֵׂית מִן הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ:


ינתנו לאומנין בשכרן – and they go out to the status of “non-holy,” even though no other thing enters in its place, for he holds that something dedicated to the Temple can be redeemed through service as it is written (Exodus 25:8): “And let them make Me a sanctuary [that I may dwell among them],” that the service/work will be accomplished from that which is donated to the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:6:4)


**אֵינָהּ מִן הַמִּדָּה.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵין מִדָּה זוֹ שֶׁאַתָּה אוֹמֵר כַּמִּדָּה הָאֲמוּרָה לְעֵיל בַּקְּטֹרֶת, וְאֵין רָאוּי אֶלָּא שֶׁתַּשְׁוֶה מִדּוֹתֶיךָ. הִלְכָּךְ מַפְרִישִׁים מֵהֶן שְׂכַר הָאֻמָּנִים כוּ'‏ כִּדְאָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל גַּבֵּי מוֹתַר הַקְּטֹרֶת. שֶׁאֵין הֶקְדֵּשׁ מִתְחַלֵּל עַל הַמְּלָאכָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּבֶן עַזַּאי:


אינה מן המדה – that is to say, that this measure that you are stating is not like the measure mentioned above with the incense and it is not appropriate but rather that your qualities should be harmonized. Therefore, we separate out the payment of the artisans, etc., as we stated above concerning the surplus of the incense for whatever is dedicated to the Temple is not redeemed through service, and the Halakha is according to Ben Azzai.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:7:1)


**זְכָרִים יִמָּכְרוּ לְצָרְכֵי עוֹלוֹת כוּ'‏ וּדְמֵיהֶם יִפְּלוּ עִם שְׁאָר הַנְּכָסִים לְבֶדֶק הַבָּיִת.** דְּסָבַר סְתָם הֶקְדֵּשׁ לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת, וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמִידֵי דַּחֲזִי לַמִּזְבֵּחַ, אֲבָל הָרָאוּי לַמִּזְבֵּחַ אֵינוֹ יוֹצֵא מִידֵי מִזְבֵּחַ, שֶׁהַמַּקְדִּישׁ תְּמִימִים לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת אֵין נִפְדִּים אֶלָּא לַמִּזְבֵּחַ, וְהַדָּמִים יִפְּלוּ לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת:


זכרים ימכרו לצרכי עולות כו' ודמיהם יפלו לשאר הנכסים לבדק הבית – for he holds that undefined things dedicated to the Temple is for the repair and upkeep of the Temple and even with something that is appropriate for the Altar, but that which is appropriate for the Altar does not leave from the Altar for someone who dedicates pure things to the repair and upkeep of the Temple are not redeemed other than for the Altar and their monies will fall to the repair and upkeep of the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:7:2)


**וְיָבִיא בִדְמֵיהֶן עוֹלוֹת.** קָסָבַר מִידֵי דַּחֲזִי לְמִזְבֵּחַ מִסְתָמָא לַמִּזְבֵּחַ אַקְדְּשִׁינְהוּ, הִלְכָּךְ זְכָרִים עַצְמָן יִקָּרְבוּ עוֹלוֹת, וּנְקֵבוֹת יִמָּכְרוּ לְצָרְכֵי שְׁלָמִים וְיָבִיא בִּדְמֵיהֶן עוֹלוֹת, אֲבָל הֵן עַצְמָן אֵין קְרֵבִין שְׁלָמִים, דְּהַמַּקְדִּישׁ נְכָסָיו דַּעְתּוֹ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כֻּלָּם לְגָבוֹהַּ, הִלְכָּךְ בְּהֵמוֹת שֶׁרְאוּיוֹת לִקָּרֵב עוֹלוֹת יִקָּרְבוּ עוֹלוֹת, וּנְקֵבוֹת יִמָּכְרוּ לְצָרְכֵי שְׁלָמִים וְיָבִיא בִּדְמֵיהֶן עוֹלוֹת, דְּכֵיוָן דִּרְאוּיוֹת לַמִּזְבֵּחַ חָלָה עֲלֵיהֶן קְדֻשַּׁת מִזְבֵּחַ, וַאֲפִלּוּ עַל הַנְּקֵבוֹת שֶׁאֵין גּוּפָן רָאוּי לְמַה שֶּׁדַּעְתּוֹ לְהַקְדִּישָׁן, כֵּיוָן דִּלְשֵׁם קָרְבָּן מִיהָא חַזְיָין, לֹא חָל עֲלֵיהֶן קְדֻשַּׁת בֶּדֶק הַבַּיִת, וְיָבִיא בִּדְמֵיהֶם עוֹלוֹת:


ויביא בדמיהן עולות – for he holds that a thing that is appropriate for the Altar without any qualification is sanctified for the Altar; therefore, males themselves should be offered as burnt offerings and females should be sold for the needs of peace-offerings and he can bring with their monies burnt offerings, but they themselves are not offered as peace offerings, for a person who dedicates his possessions, his intention is that it should all be for “On High,” therefore, cattle which are appropriate to be offered as burnt offerings should be offered as burnt offerings. But females should be sold for the needs of peace-offerings and you can, with their monies, bring burnt-offerings, for since they are appropriate for the Altar, the sanctity of the Altar applies to them. And even for the females, where their bodies are not appropriate, for what he intends to sanctify them for, since it is for the sake of a sacrifice, however, they are worthy, [but] the sanctify of the repair/maintenance of the Temple does not apply to them and he should bring with their monies burnt-offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:7:3)


**שֶׁהַמַּקְדִּישׁ בְּפֵרוּשׁ.** שֶׁאָמַר בְּהֵמָה וּנְכָסַי לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ. דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁהִפְרִישָׁן וְהִבְדִּילָן זוֹ מִזּוֹ וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן לֹא אָמַר בְּהֵמָה לַמִּזְבֵּחַ וּנְכָסַי לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת, שְׁמַע מִנַּהּ דְּדַעְתּוֹ הָיָה שֶׁבֵּין בְּהֵמָה וּבֵין נְכָסָיו הַכֹּל יֵלֵךְ אֶל מָקוֹם אֶחָד. אֲבָל הַמַּקְדִּישׁ סְתָם כָּל נְכָסָיו, דַּעְתּוֹ לְהַקְדִּישׁ כָּל דָּבָר לְמַה שֶּׁהוּא רָאוּי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


המקדיש בפירוש – that said that cattle and my possessions are given over to the Temple property for since he dedicated them and separated them one from the other, and even though he didn’t say, the cattle to the altar and my possessions to the upkeep and maintenance of the Temple, it comes to tell us that his intention was that regardless of whether speaking of his cattle or his possession everything should go to one place. But when one sanctifies something all of his possessions undefined, his intention is to dedicate each thing for what is appropriate, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:8:1)


**יֵינוֹת שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת.** שֶׁהֵן רְאוּיִים לִמְנָחוֹת וְלִנְסָכִים:


יינות שמנים וסלתות – which are appropriate for meal-offerings and libations.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:8:2)


**וְעוֹפוֹת.** תּוֹרִים וּבְנֵי יוֹנָה:


ועופות – doves and pigeons.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:8:3)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר.** גַּרְסִינַן, וְלֹא רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, וְטַעֲמָא דְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר מְפֹרָשׁ בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) לְכָל נִדְרֵיהֶם וּלְכָל נִדְבוֹתֵיהֶם אֲשֶׁר יַקְרִיבוּ לַה'‏ לְעוֹלָה, כָּל מַה שֶּׁהֵם נוֹדְרִים וּמִתְנַדְּבִים מִדְּבָרִים אֲשֶׁר יַקְרִיבוּ לַה', אֲפִלּוּ יֵינוֹת שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת, יִהְיֶה לְעוֹלָה. יָכוֹל יַקְרִיב בִּדְמֵיהֶן עוֹלַת הָעוֹף, אוֹ אִם הִקְדִּישׁ עוֹף יַקְרִיבֶנּוּ עוֹלָה, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר (שם) בַּבָּקָר בַּכְּשָׂבִים וּבָעִזִּים, הָא אֵינוֹ מַקְרִיב בְּכָל מַה שֶּׁהִתְנַדֵּב אֶלָּא עוֹלַת בְּהֵמָה בִּלְבַד:


Rabbi Eleazar is our reading and not Rabbi Eliezer. The reason of Rabbi Eleazar is explained in the Jerusalem Talmud, as it is written (Leviticus 22:18): “[When any man of the house of Israel or of the strangers in Israel] presents a burnt offering as his offering for any of the votive or any of the freewill offerings [that they offer to the LORD],” all that they vow or donate from things that they donate to the LOD, even wines, oils and fine-flour will be for a burnt offering – he is able to offer with their monetary value a burnt offering of fowl or if he first gave a fowl, he shall offer it as a burnt offering, the inference teaches us (Leviticus 22:19): “from cattle or sheep or goats.” Surely, he does not with all what he donated other than only a burnt offering of cattle.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:1)


**מְשַׁעֲרִין אֶת הַלִּשְׁכָּה.** פּוֹסְקִים שַׁעַר לְיֵינוֹת שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת, שַׁעַר שֶׁיַּעֲמֹד זֶה הַשַּׁעַר שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם. וְלוֹקְחִים בְּכָל יוֹם מִמּוֹכְרֵי יֵינוֹת שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת מַה שֶּׁהֵן צְרִיכִין בְּאוֹתוֹ הַסְּכוּם שֶׁפָּסְקוּ, וְאִם נִתְיַקֵּר הַמִּקָּח אֵין מוֹסִיפִים דָּמִים, וְאִם הוּזַל לוֹקְחִים לְפִי הַזּוֹל:


משערין את הלשכה – they calculate the market price for wines, oils and fine-flour and this market price will stand for thirty days. And they purchase every day from the sellers of wine, oils and fine flour what they need for that sum that they established. But if the cost went up, they do no t add monies, and if it became less expensive, they purchase according to the cheaper price.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:2)


**הַמְקַבֵּל עָלָיו לְסַפֵּק סְלָתוֹת.** בִּימוֹת קְצִיר חִטִּים וּבְצִירַת עֲנָבִים וּמְסִיקַת זֵיתִים הָיוּ הַגִּזְבָּרִים מַקְדִּימִים מָעוֹת לַחֶנְוָנִי, וְהָיָה הַחֶנְוָנִי מְקַבֵּל עָלָיו לְסַפֵּק יֵינוֹת שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת כָּל הַשָּׁנָה. וְאִם הָיוּ אָז נִמְכָּרִים אַרְבַּע סְאִין בְּסֶלַע וְהוּקְרוּ וְעָמְדוּ שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִין בְּסֶלַע, צָרִיךְ לִתֵּן אַרְבַּע סְאִין, דְּהֶקְדֵּשׁ קוֹנֶה בְּכֶסֶף, דִּכְתִיב וְנָתַן הַכֶּסֶף וְקָם לוֹ. וְאִם קִבֵּל לְסַפֵּק שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִין בְּסֶלַע וְהוּזְלוּ וְעָמְדוּ אַרְבַּע בְּסֶלַע, נוֹתֵן אַרְבַּע בְּסֶלַע, דְּלֹא גָּרַע מֵהֶדְיוֹט דְּלֹא קָנֵי אֶלָּא בִּמְשִׁיכָה:


המקבל עליו לספק סלתות – in the days of the wheat harvest and the vintage of the grapes and olive harvest, the treasurers would advance money to the storekeeper and the storekeeper would accept upon himself to supply wine, oils and fine flour all year long. But if they were then sold four Se’ah for a Sela and their price increased and stood at three Se’ah for a Sela, one must give four Se’ah. For that which is dedicated to a sacred purpose/Temple property acquires with money, as it is written (Leviticus 27:19 – though the quote is not exact, but all of the sources refer to this verse): “[And if he who consecrated the land wishes to redeem it, he must add one-fifth to the sum at which it was assessed,] and it shall pass to him.” But if he accepted to supply three Se’ah for a Sela and the price became cheaper and stood at four [Se’ah] for a Selah, he gives the four [Se’ah] for a Selah, for he is not worse than a commoner, that does not acquire other than through משיכה/”pulling.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:3)


**הִתְלִיעַ לוֹ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ מָשַׁךְ הַגִּזְבָּר וְנָתַן מָעוֹת, אַחֲרָיוּתָם עַל הַחֶנְוָנִי:


התליע לו – even if the treasurer pulled and gave the monies for the property which may be resorted to in case of non-payment on behalf of the storekeeper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:4)


**אֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל אֶת מְעוֹתָיו.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵינָם נֶחֱשָׁבִים מְעוֹתָיו שֶׁל חֶנְוָנִי וְלֹא זָכָה בָּהֶם, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁקִּבְּלָם מִן הַגִּזְבָּר:


אינו מקבל את מעותיו – that is to say, they are not considered the monies of the storekeeper nor did he take possession of them, and even though he received them from the treasurer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 4:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/4:9:5)


**עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מְרַצֶּה.** שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה לְרָצוֹן עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ. הִלְכָּךְ אִם הֶחֱמִיץ הַיַּיִן אוֹ הִתְלִיעָה הַסֹּלֶת, אַחֲרָיוּתָם עַל הַחֶנְוָנִי:


עד שיהיה המזבח מרצה – Therefore, if the wine soured or the fine-flour became infected with worms, the responsibility is upon is the storekeeper.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:1)


**אֵלּוּ הֵן הַמְמֻנִּים.** חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר מִינִים שֶׁל פְּקִידוּת וּמִנּוּי שֶׁל שְׂרָרָה דְּחָשִׁיב בְּמַתְנִיתִין הָיוּ תָּמִיד בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ. מִיהוּ הָנָךְ גַּבְרֵי דְּחָשִׁיב, לֹא בִּזְמַן אֶחָד הָיוּ, אֶלָּא הַחֲסִידִים וְהַכְּשֵׁרִים שֶׁבְּכָל דּוֹר וָדוֹר הוּא מוֹנֶה. וְרַבּוֹתַי פֵּרְשׁוּ, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַמְּמֻנִּים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים שֶׁנִּתְמַנּוּ עַל כָּךְ, כָּךְ הָיָה שְׁמָם, לְפִיכָךְ נִקְרְאוּ הַבָּאִים אַחֲרֵיהֶם עַל שְׁמָם:


אלו הן הממונים – fifteen kinds of officers and appointment to authority that are considered in our Mishnah were the daily offering in the Temple. However, these men that are considered were not at the same time, but the pious and the worthy in each generation he would appoint. But my teachers explained on account that the first appointees were appointed for this – thus this was their names. Therefore, the ones who came after them were called by their name


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:2)


**עַל הַחוֹתָמוֹת וְעַל הַסְּלָתוֹת.** מְפֹרָשׁ לְקַמָּן בְּמַתְנִיתִין:


על החותמות ועל הסלתות – it will be explained further in our Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:3)


**עַל הַפְּיָסוֹת.** לְהוֹרוֹת סֵדֶר הַגּוֹרָל מִי יִזְכֶּה בָּעֲבוֹדָה זוֹ וּמִי בְּזוֹ, כִּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת יוֹמָא:


על הפייסות – to teach the order of the lottery, who will merit with this service, and whom with that, as is explained in the Tractate Yoma (see Chapter 2, Mishnayot 1-4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:4)


**עַל הַקִּנִּים.** מְחֻסְּרֵי כַּפָּרָה כְּגוֹן זָב וְזָבָה וְיוֹלֶדֶת שֶׁמְּבִיאִים קִנֵּי חוֹבָה, דְּהַיְנוּ תּוֹרִים וּבְנֵי יוֹנָה, נוֹתְנִין מְעוֹתֵיהֶן לַשּׁוֹפָרוֹת שֶׁבַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְהַמְּמֻנִּים עַל הַשּׁוֹפָרוֹת לוֹקְחִים הַמָּעוֹת וּמְבִיאִין בָּהֵן הַקִּנִּים. וְהָיָה צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתְמַנֶּה עַל זֶה חָכָם גָּדוֹל וּבָקִי, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּמַסֶּכֶת אָבוֹת (פ"ג מי"ח) קִנִּים וּפִתְחֵי נִדָּה הֵן הֵן גּוּפֵי הֲלָכוֹת:


על הקינים – those lacking atonement such as the man and/or a woman with a flux and a woman who just gave birth bring obligatory bird-offerings which are doves and pigeons – they put their monies in the shofar-shaped [chests] that are in the Temple. But those who are appointed over the shofar-shaped [chests] take the money and bring through them the bird-offerings, and someone who was wise, great in knowledge and a specialist had to be appointed, as we state in Tractate Avot (Chapter 3, Mishnah 18).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:5)


**שֶׁהָיָה פּוֹתֵחַ דְּבָרִים כוּ'.** וְהוּא מָרְדְּכַי בִּלְשָׁן שֶׁעָלָה מִן הַגּוֹלָה. וְנִקְרָא כֵּן, שֶׁהָיָה בּוֹלֵל לְשׁוֹנוֹת הַרְבֵּה:


שהיה פותח דברים וכו' – This is Mordecai Bilshan that came up [to the land of Israel] from the Diaspora, and he is called this because he mixed up many languages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:6)


**עַל חוֹלֵי מֵעַיִם.** לְפִי שֶׁהַכֹּהֲנִים הוֹלְכִים יְחֵפִים עַל הָרִצְפָּה וְאוֹכְלִין בָּשָׂר הַרְבֵּה וְשׁוֹתִין מַיִם, הָיוּ מֵעֵיהֶן מִתְקַלְקְלִין, וּצְרִיכִין תָּמִיד לְרוֹפֵא לוֹמַר לָהֶם זֶה הַסַּם טוֹב לַמֵּעַיִם:


על חולי מעים – because the Kohanim walk barefoot on the floor and eat a lot of meat and drink water, their intestines were ruined and they always needed a doctor to tell them: this is the good medication for the intestines.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:7)


**חוֹפֵר שִׁיחִין.** הָיָה מְמֻנֶּה לַחְפֹּר בּוֹרוֹת שִׁיחִין וּמְעָרוֹת, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מַיִם מְצוּיִין לִשְׁתּוֹת לְעוֹלֵי רְגָלִים:


חופר שיחין – he was appointed to dig cisterns, pits and caves, in order that water would be found to drink for those who come up [to Jerusalem] on the Pilgrimage Festivals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:8)


**גְּבִינִי כָּרוֹז.** כָּךְ שְׁמוֹ שֶׁל הָאִישׁ הַמַּכְרִיז בְּכָל בֹּקֶר בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ עִמְדוּ כֹּהֲנִים לַעֲבוֹדַתְכֶם. וְשׁוֹמְעִים קוֹלוֹ מִיְּרִיחוֹ:


גביני כרוז – that was his name of the man who would announce each morning in the Temple: “Arise, O Priests, to your Divine Service” and they would hear his voice from Jericho.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:9)


**עַל נְעִילַת שְׁעָרִים.** לִנְעֹל הַשְּׁעָרִים בָּעֶרֶב וּלְפָתְחָן בַּבֹּקֶר:


על נעילת שערים – to lock the gates in the evening and to open them in the morning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:10)


**עַל הַפָּקִיעַ.** לְהַלְקוֹת הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ יְשֵׁנִים עַל מִשְׁמְרוֹתֵיהֶם, שֶׁהָיוּ שׁוֹמְרִים אֶת הַמִּקְדָּשׁ בַּלַּיְלָה, כִּדְאָמְרָן בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (פ"א מ"ב) מִי שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹצְאִים אוֹתוֹ יָשֵׁן הָיוּ חוֹבְטִים אוֹתוֹ וְשׂוֹרְפִים אֶת כְּסוּתוֹ. וּבַיְרוּשַׁלְמִי מְפֹרָשׁ, הַפָּקִיעַ, הַפְּתִילוֹת שֶׁל מְנוֹרָה וְשֶׁל בֵּית הַשּׁוֹאֵבָה, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק הֶחָלִיל (סכה פ"ה מ"ג) מִבְּלָאֵי מִכְנְסֵי כֹּהֲנִים וּמֵהֶמְיָנֵיהֶם הָיוּ מַפְקִיעִים פְּתִילוֹת:


על הפקיע – to whip the Kohanim and Levites that were found to be sleeping during their watches when they would guard the Temple at night, as we say in Tractate [Middot] (see Chapter 1, Mishnah 2, where it states that would use a stick – and not necessarily a strip of leather/strap used as a whip), whomever they would find sleeping they would beat him and burn his clothing. But in the Jerusalem Talmud, they would explain that he stripped the wicks of the Menorah and of the House of Water Drawing as we state in [Tractate Sukkah], the chapter “The flute-playing” (chapter 5) [Talmud Sukkah 51b -see Mishnah 3] – “from the warn-out pants and girdles of the Kohanim, they made wicks [and would kindle them].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:11)


**צִלְצָל.** כְּלִי שִׁיר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ קוֹל גָּדוֹל, לָשׁוֹן תְּצִלֶּינָה אָזְנָיו, צימבל"ו בָּלַע"ז. וּכְשֶׁהָיוּ שׁוֹמְעִין קוֹלוֹ הָיוּ הַלְוִיִּם מַתְחִילִין בְּשִׁיר:


צלצל – a musical instrument that has a large sound, from the language תצילנה אזניו/it will be a loud shriek in his ears, “cymbal” in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:12)


**עַל הַשִּׁיר.** לְהַתְחִיל אֶת הַשִּׁיר, וּכְשֶׁהָיָה מְסַיֵּם הָיוּ כֻּלָּן מְסַיְּמִין:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:13)


**בֵּית גַּרְמוּ.** שֵׁם מִשְׁפָּחָה:


בית גרמו – the name of a family.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:14)


**עַל לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים.** שֶׁהָיָה עָשׂוּי כְּמִין תֵּבָה פְּרוּצָה, וְהָיוּ אֻמָּנִים בַּעֲשִׂיָּתוֹ וּבַאֲפִיָּתוֹ לִרְדּוֹתוֹ מִן הַתַּנּוּר שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁבֵר וְלַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִתְעַפֵּשׁ:


על לחם הפנים – that would be made something like a breached chest and they were artisans in his action and in his baking to detach it from the oven so that it would not break and to make it so that it would not grow moldy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:15)


**בֵּיִת אַבְטִינָס.** שֵׁם מִשְׁפָּחָה שֶׁהָיוּ בְּקִיאִים לְפַטֵּם הַקְּטֹרֶת, וּמַכִּירִין בְּעֵשֶׂב שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ מַעֲלֶה עָשָׁן, וּכְשֶׁהָיוּ מְעָרְבִין אוֹתוֹ עִם סַמְמָנֵי הַקְּטֹרֶת הָיָה עֲשַׁן הַקְּטֹרֶת מִתַּמֵּר וְעוֹלֶה כְּמִין מַקֵּל:


בית אבטינס – the name of a family that were experts in mixing the incense and they recognized a certain grass whose name was a smoke-raiser (name of a plant) and when they would mix it with the spices of the incense, the smoke of the incense would rise straight up like a stick.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:16)


**עַל הַפָּרֹכוֹת.** לַעֲשׂוֹת פָּרֹכוֹת חֲדָשׁוֹת כְּשֶׁהָיוּ צְרִיכִים:


על הפרכות – to make new hangings when they were needed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:1:17)


**הַמַּלְבִּישׁ.** הָיָה מְמֻנֶּה לְהַלְבִּישׁ הַכֹּהֲנִים בִּשְׁעַת עֲבוֹדָה וּלְהַפְשִׁיטָם אַחַר עֲבוֹדָה, וְלִשְׁמֹר בִּגְדֵי כְּהֻנָּה בַּלְּשָׁכוֹת הָעֲשׂוּיוֹת לְכָךְ:


המלביש – he was appointed to dress the Kohanim at the time of the Divine Service and to undress them after their service, and to preserve the priestly garments in the compartments that were designed for this.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:2:1)


**גִּזְבָּרִים.** הֵן שֶׁמָּמוֹן הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ תַּחַת יָדָם, וְהֵן פּוֹדִים הָעֳרְכִין וְהַחֲרָמִין וְהַהַקְדָּשׁוֹת, וְכָל מְלֶאכֶת הַקֹּדֶשׁ בָּהֶם נַעֲשִׂים:


גזברים – they are those under whose control are the sacred property of the Temple, and they redeem the valuation/assessment due of a person or animal dedicated to the Temple and the property set apart for the Temple’s usage and that which is sacred property of the Temple and all of the holy tasks that are performed through them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:2:2)


**אֲמַרְכָּלִין.** לְמַעְלָה מִן הַגִּזְבָּרִים. וּנְשִׂיא נְשִׂיאֵי הַלֵּוִי (במדבר ג) מְתַרְגְּמִינַן וַאֲמַרְכָּלַיָא דְמָמוֹנָא. וּלְשׁוֹן אֲמַרְכָּל, אָמַר עַל כָּל. וּבְעַרְבִי קוֹרִים לְגָדוֹל אֱמִיר. מֶה הָיוּ שִׁבְעָה אֲמַרְכָּלִין עוֹשִׂין, שִׁבְעָה מַפְתְּחוֹת הָעֲזָרָה בְּיָדָם, רָצָה אֶחָד מֵהֶן לִפְתֹּחַ אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל עַד שֶׁיִּכָּנְסוּ כֻּלָּם וְיִפְתְּחוּ, וְהַגִּזְבָּרִים נִכְנָסִים אַחֲרֵיהֶם וּמוֹצִיאִים מַה שֶּׁהֵן צְרִיכִין. וְעוֹד מַעֲלָה הָאַחֶרֶת לְמַעְלָה מִן הָאֲמַרְכָּלִים הָיְתָה שָׁם, שֶׁלֹּא נִזְכְּרָה בַּמִּשְׁנָה וּמַיְתֵי לַהּ בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא, וְהֵן שְׁנֵי קְתִילוֹקִין. וְנִמְצְאוּ שָׁם חָמֵשׁ מַדְרֵגוֹת, כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל, סְגָן, קְתִילוֹקִין, אֲמַרְכָּלִין, גִּזְבָּרִים:


אמרכלין – they are higher than the treasurers and (Numbers 3:32): “The head chieftain of the Levites [was Eleazar son of Aaron the priest, in charge of those attending to the duties of the sanctuary],” which we translate [in Aramaic] as "ואמרכלין דממנא" and the language "אמרכל" - “he stated over everything,” and in Arabic we call him “Amir.” What did the seven officers/אמרכלין do? Seven keys of the courtyard were in their hands. If one of them wanted to open it up, he could not until all of them entered in together and the treasurers coming after them would open it and remove whatever they needed. And another level above that of the אמרכלין/officer/trustees (superintending the cashiers) was there, which was not mentioned in the Mishnah and it is brought in the Tosefta (Chapter 2, Halakha 15 – Lieberman edition), and they are two financial officers/controllers ("קתילוקין" ) and there were found five levels there: The High Priest, his assistant, the financial controllers, the trustees, treasurers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:2:3)


**אֵין עוֹשִׂין שְׂרָרָה פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁנַיִם.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כח) וְהֵם יִקְּחוּ אֶת הַזָּהָב, מִעוּט רַבִּים שְׁנַיִם:


ואין עושין שררה פחות משנים – as it is written (Exodus 28:5): “they, therefore shall receive the gold, [the blue, purple, and crimson yarns and the fine linen]”; the least of many is two.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:1)


**אַרְבָּעָה חוֹתָמוֹת הָיוּ בַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** שָׁלֹשׁ חוֹתָמוֹת לִשְׁלֹשָׁה נְסָכִין חֲלוּקִים שֶׁל בְּהֵמוֹת. חוֹתָם רִאשׁוֹן לְנִסְכֵּי בָּקָר, וְהֵן שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים סֹלֶת בָּלוּל בַּחֲצִי הַהִין שֶׁמֶן, וְיַיִן לְנֶסֶךְ חֲצִי הַהִין, וְחוֹתָמָן עֵגֶל. וְהַשֵּׁנִי זָכָר לְנִסְכֵּי אַיִל, שֶׁתַּרְגּוּם אַיִל דִּכְרָא, וּנְסָכָיו שְׁתֵּי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים סֹלֶת בָּלוּל בַּשֶּׁמֶן שְׁלִישִׁית הַהִין, וְיַיִן לַנֶּסֶךְ שְׁלִישִׁית הַהִין. וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי גְּדִי, לְנִסְכֵּי כֶּבֶשׂ בֶּן שְׁנָתוֹ, וְהֵן עִשָּׂרוֹן סֹלֶת בָּלוּל בִּרְבִיעִית הַהִין שֶׁמֶן, וְיַיִן לַנֶּסֶךְ רְבִיעִית הַהִין. וְהָרְבִיעִי חוֹטֵא, וְהֵם נְסָכָיו שֶׁל מְצֹרָע עָשִׁיר שֶׁמֵּבִיא שָׁלֹשׁ בְּהֵמוֹת וְצָרִיךְ עֲשָׂרָה לֻגִּין שֶׁמֶן, תִּשְׁעָה לִשְׁלֹשָׁה כְּבָשִׂים וְאֶחָד לְמַתַּן תְּנוּךְ וּבְהוֹנוֹת. וְקָרִי לֵיהּ חוֹטֵא, מִשּׁוּם דְּאָמְרִינַן בַּעֲרָכִין (טז.) עַל שִׁבְעָה דְּבָרִים נְגָעִים בָּאִין:


ד' חותמות היו במקדש – three seals for three libations [connected with animal sacrifices] that are divided with the cattle . The first seal was for libations for cattle and they are three-tenths of an Ephah of fine flour mingled with one-half a Hin of oil and one-half a Hin of wine for libation and their seal was an עגל/calf. And the second was a ram for libations of a ram – as the Aramaic translation of איל/ram isדכרא /ram and its libation is two-tenths of a fine flour mingled in one-third a Hin of oil and one-third a Hin of wine for libation. And the third was a kid for libations of a sheep one-year old and they were one Issaron (i.e., one-tenth of an Ephah) of fine flour mingled with one-quarter of a Hin of oil and one-quarter of a Hin of wine for libation. And the fourth was “sinner,” and they were libations of the rich leper that would bring three cattle and it requires ten Log of pure oil for the three sheep and to place on the earlobe and the thumbs, and they call it "חוטא" /”sinner” because we state in Tractate Arakhin (16a): “For seven things plagues come: [gossip, murder, false oaths, illicit sexual behavior; haughtiness, theft, and selfishness].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:2)


**חֲמִשָּׁה הָיוּ.** שְׁנֵי חוֹתָמוֹת הָיוּ לַמְצֹרָע:


חמשה היו – to seals were for the leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:3)


**חוֹטֵא דַל וְחוֹטֵא עָשִׁיר.** לְפִי שֶׁמְּצֹרָע עָנִי אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶלָּא בְּהֵמָה אַחַת. וְאִם לֹא הָיָה לַמְּצֹרָע אֶלָּא חוֹתָם אֶחָד, הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים לִמְצֹרָע עָנִי שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר לִמְצֹרָע עָנִי נוֹתְנִים לוֹ חוֹתַם גְּדִי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


חוטא דל וחוטא עשיר – because a poor leper is unable to bring other than one cow, and if there was only one seal for a leper, they would give to the poor leper three-tenths, but the first Tanna/teacher held that the for the poor leper, they would give him the seal of the “kid,”/"גדי" and the Halakha is according to the first Tanna.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:4)


**וַאֲרָמִית כָּתוּב עֲלֵיהֶן.** שֶׁרֹב דִּבּוּרָם בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי הָיָה:


וארמית כתוב עליהן – for most of their speech was in the Aramaic language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:3:5)


**זְכָרִים וּנְקֵבוֹת.** בֵּין לְנִסְכֵּי עוֹלוֹת שֶׁאֵינָן בָּאוֹת אֶלָּא זְכָרִים, בֵּין לְנִסְכֵּי שְׁלָמִים שֶׁבָּאִים זְכָרִים אוֹ נְקֵבוֹת. וְעוֹלוֹת וּשְׁלָמִים הֵן שֶׁטְּעוּנִין נְסָכִים, כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר טו) עוֹלָה אוֹ זֶבַח:


זכרים ונקבות – whether for libations of burnt offerings that don’t come other than from males or whether from libations of peace-offerings that come from males or females, for the burnt offerings and and peace offerings are those that require libations, as it is written (Numbers 15:3): “[and would present an offering by fire to the LORD from the herd or from the flock,] be it burnt offering or sacrifice, [in fulfillment of a vow explicitly uttered, or as a freewill offering, or at your fixed occasions, producing an odor pleasing to the LORD].”



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:4:1)


**נוֹתֵן לוֹ מָעוֹת.** כְּפִי הַנְּסָכִים שֶׁהוּא צָרִיךְ:


נותן לו מעות – according to the libations that he needs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:4:2)


**בָּא לוֹ אֵצֶל אֲחִיָּה שֶׁהָיָה מְמֻנֶּה.** לִקְנוֹת נְסָכִים, יֵינוֹת שְׁמָנִים וּסְלָתוֹת, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא צָרִיךְ כָּל מֵבִיא קָרְבָּן לְחַזֵּר אַחַר נְסָכִים הָעֲשׂוּיִין עַל טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


בא לו אצל אחיה שהיה ממונה – to purchase libations of the wines, oils, and sifted fine flours, in order that everyone who brings a sacrifice would not have to go back after the libations that are used for the purification of that which has been dedicated to the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:4:3)


**וְאִם הוֹתִירוּ הוֹתִירוּ לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא מְעוֹתָיו שֶׁל יוֹחָנָן הֵם שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ בְּמָעוֹת שׁ?ֶל נְסָכִים:


ואם הותירו הותירו להקדש – and we don’t say lest these monies were Yohanan’s that were mingled with the monies of the libations.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:5:1)


**מַמְתִּינִין לוֹ עַד הָעָרֶב.** שֶׁיִּזְדַּוְּגוּ יַחַד יוֹחָנָן וַאֲחִיָּה, אִם נִמְצְאוּ מָעוֹת יְתֵרִים בְּיַד יוֹחָנָן כְּנֶגֶד הַחוֹתָם שֶׁאוֹמֵר זֶה שֶׁנֶּאֱבַד מִמֶּנּוּ, נוֹתְנִים לוֹ:


ממתינים לו עד הערב – Yohanan and Ahiyah join together, if they found excess monies in the hand on Yohanan corresponding to the seal that [if] one states that he lost it, and they give it to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:5:2)


**וְשֵׁם הַיּוֹם כָּתוּב.** עַל הַחוֹתָם, יוֹם פְּלוֹנִי וְחֹדֶשׁ פְּלוֹנִי:


ושם היום כתוב – on the seal, a certain day and a certain month.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:5:3)


**מִפְּנֵי הָרַמָּאִין.** שֶׁמָּא מָצָא חוֹתָם שֶׁנָּפַל מֵחֲבֵרוֹ אוֹ מֵאֲחִיָּה אוֹ מִיּוֹחָנָן, וּבָא עַכְשָׁיו לִקַּח, וּבַיּוֹם שֶׁמְּצָאוֹ אֵינוֹ רַשַּׁאי לְהוֹצִיאוֹ, שֶׁהָאוֹבֵד מְחַזֵּר אַחֲרָיו. וְעוֹד יֵשׁ לָחוּשׁ שֶׁמָּא לָקַח חוֹתָם כְּשַׁעַר הַזּוֹל, וְהִצְנִיעוֹ עַד שֶׁנִּתְיַקֵּר הַשַּׁעַר:


מפני הרמאין – lest some found a seal that fell from his fell, either from Ahiyah or from Yohanan, and he comes now to take it. But on the day that he finds it, he is not permitted to take it out, as the person who lost it searches after it. And furthermore, one must suspect that lest he purchased the seal at the lowest market price and hid it until the market price became more expensive.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:6:1)


**לִשְׁכַּת חֲשָׁאִים.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַנּוֹתְנִים בְּתוֹכָהּ מָעוֹת נוֹתְנִים בְּסֵתֶר, וְהַמִּתְפַּרְנְסִים מִמֶּנָּה נוֹטְלִים בְּסֵתֶר:


לשכת חשאים – on account that those who give money into it do so in secrecy and those who are supported from it take it in secrecy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/5:6:2)


**לְלִשְׁכַּת בֶּדֶק הַבַּיִת.** לַלִּשְׁכָּה שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בָּהּ כָּל קָדְשֵׁי בֶּדֶק הַבָּיִת:


ללשכת בדק הבית – to the chamber where they place in it all the holy [things] for Temple repairs.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:1:1)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר שׁוֹפָרוֹת.** תֵּבוֹת צָרוֹת מִלְּמַעְלָה וּרְחָבוֹת מִלְּמַטָּה עֲקֻמּוֹת כְּעֵין שׁוֹפָר, מִפְּנֵי הָרַמָּאִים שֶׁלֹּא יוּכְלוּ לְהַכְנִיס יָדָן לְתוֹכָן לְהַרְאוֹת עַצְמָן כְּאִלּוּ נוֹתְנִין לְתוֹכָן, וְנוֹטְלִין מִתּוֹכָן. וּלְקַמָּן מְפָרֵשׁ לָמָּה שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר שׁוֹפָרוֹת וּשְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר שֻׁלְחָנוֹת, וּבְאֵיזֶה מָקוֹם מֻנָּחִים:


שלשה עשר שופרות – [thirteen] chests, narrow from the top and wide from the bottom curved like a Shofar because of the deceivers who would not be able to put in their hands into them to show themselves as if they are giving/putting into it, while they are taking from it, and further on, it will explain why there are thirteen chests and thirteen tables and in which location they are placed (see also Mishnah 5 in this chapter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:1:2)


**שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה הִשְׁתַּחֲוָיוֹת.** לְקַמָּן מְפָרֵשׁ הֵיכָן הָיוּ:


שלש עשרה השתחויות – and further on (Mishnah 3) it will explain where they were.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:1:3)


**כְּנֶגֶד דִּיר הָעֵצִים.** לִשְׁכָּה שֶׁשָּׁם הָיוּ אוֹצְרִים כָּל עֲצֵי הַמַּעֲרָכָה, וְהִיא הָיְתָה בְּמִקְצוֹעַ מִזְרָחִית צְפוֹנִית שֶׁל עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים:


כנגד דיר העצים – a chamber/compartment (see also Tractate Middot, Chapter 5, Mishnah 4) where they would store there all the wood of the pile of wood on the altar of the Temple and it was in the northeast corner of the Women’s Court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:1:4)


**שֶׁשָּׁם הָאָרוֹן נִגְנַז.** שֶׁיֹּאשִׁיָּהוּ הַמֶּלֶךְ צִוָּה וּגְנָזוּהוּ לְמַטָּה בְּמַטְמוֹנִיּוֹת עֲקֻמּוֹת וַעֲקַלְקַלּוֹת שֶׁבָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה בִּזְמַן שֶׁבָּנָה אֶת הַבַּיִת וְיָדַע שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ לֵחָרֵב. וְהַיְנוּ דִּכְתִיב בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (ב לה) בְּיֹאשִׁיָּהוּ, וַיֹּאמֶר לַלְוִיִּם הַמְּבִינִים [וְגוֹ'] תְּנוּ אֶת אֲרוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ בַּבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר בָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה וְכוּ'. וְעִמּוֹ נִגְנַז מַטֵּה אַהֲרֹן, וְצִנְצֶנֶת הַמָּן, וּצְלוֹחִית שֶׁל שֶׁמֶן הַמִּשְׁחָה:


ששם הארון נגנז – for King Josiah commanded that it be hidden below in the curved secret and tortuous path that Solomon built at the time when he built the Temple and knew that it would eventually be destroyed. And this is what is written in II Chronicles, chapter 35, verse 3: “He said to the Levites, consecrated to the LORD, who taught all Israel, ‘Put the Holy Ark in the House that Solomon [the son of David, king of Israel] built” (also compare Talmud Yoma 53b) and with it was hidden the staff of Aaron and the bottle of Manna (which was preserved) and the flask of anointing oil.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:2:1)


**שֶׁהָיָה מִתְעַסֵּק.** בִּמְלַאכְתּוֹ, מַתְלִיעַ אֶת הָעֵצִים, וּבַעַל מוּם הָיָה, וּמְלַאכְתָּן שֶׁל בַּעֲלֵי מוּמִין לְהָכִין עֵצִים לַמַּעֲרָכָה, וְכָל עֵץ שֶׁנִּמְצָא בּוֹ תּוֹלַעַת פָּסוּל לַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


שהיה מתעסק – in his service (see also Tractate Middot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 5) removing the wormy parts from the wood/sorting it, and he had a blemish (i.e., he was unfit for the altar, for priestly service), and the work of those with a blemish was to prepare the wood for the altar, and any wood in which was found worms was unfit/disqualified for the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:2:2)


**שֶׁהִיא מְשֻׁנָּה.** לֹא הָיְתָה הָאֶבֶן שָׁוָה לִשְׁאַר אַבְנֵי הָרִצְפָּה, וְהֵבִין שֶׁנִּסְתַּלְּקָה מִשָּׁם וְהֻחְזְרָה:


שהוא משונה – the stone was not even with the other stones of the floor, and he understood it (i.e., the Holy Ark) was taken from there and then returned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:2:3)


**בְּיִחוּד.** בְּבֵרוּר:


ביחוד – with certainty.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:1)


**וְהֵיכָן הָיוּ הִשְׁתַּחֲוָיוֹת.** שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה הִשְׁתַּחֲוָיוֹת דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל, הֵיכָן הָיוּ עוֹשִׂין אוֹתָן:


והיכן היו ההשתחויות – thirteen prostrations, as is taught in the Mishnah above (Mishnah 1 of this chapter), where they made them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:2)


**שַׁעַר הָעֶלְיוֹן.** הַר הַבַּיִת הָיָה מְשֻׁפָּע וְעוֹלֶה מִמִּזְרָח לְמַעֲרָב, וְהַשַּׁעַר הַסָּמוּךְ לְמַעֲרָב הוּא שַׁעַר הָעֶלְיוֹן. וְאַחֲרָיו שַׁעַר הַדֶּלֶק, הוּא שַׁעַר שֶׁל לִשְׁכַּת הָעֵץ שֶׁהָיְתָה בִּדְרוֹם הָעֲזָרָה, וְעַל שֵׁם שֶׁמַּכְנִיסִין דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם עֲצֵי הַמַּעֲרָכָה שֶׁדּוֹלְקִים עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ נִקְרָא שַׁעַר הַדֶּלֶק:


שער עליון – the Temple was slanted going up from east to west, and the gate nearest to the west was the Upper Gate. And behind it was the Fuel Gate, which was the gate of the wood chamber, which was in the south of the [Temple] courtyard and it was called that name because they would bring in through there the wood of the altar that they would burn on the altar which is called the Fuel Gate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:3)


**שַׁעַר הַבְּכוֹרוֹת.** שֶׁמַּכְנִיסִין שָׁם הַבְּכוֹרוֹת הַנִּשְׁחָטִים בְּדָרוֹם:


שער הבכורות – they would bring in there the first-born [cattle] that would be slaughtered there in the south.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:4)


**הַמַּיִם מְפַכִּים.** כְּדִכְתִיב בִּיחֶזְקֵאל (מז) וְהִנֵּה מַיִם מְפַכִּים מִן הַכָּתֵף הַיְּמָנִית, וְהַיְנוּ דָּרוֹם שֶׁקָּרוּי יָמִין, כְּדִכְתִיב צָפוֹן וְיָמִין. וְרָאָה יְחֶזְקֵאל בִּנְבוּאָה שֶׁהָיוּ יוֹצְאִים מִבֵּית קֹדֶשׁ הַקָּדָשִׁים דַּקִּים כְּקַרְנֵי חֲגָבִים, וּכְשֶׁמַּגִּיעִים לְשַׁעַר זֶה מִתְגַּבְּרִים וְנַעֲשִׂים כִּמְלֹא פִּי פַּךְ קָטָן, וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָרֵי לְהוּ מַיִם מְפַכִּים:


מים מפכים – as it is written in Ezekiel (47:2): “And I found water was gushing from [under] the south wall,” and that is south which is called “right-hand,” as it is written )Psalms 89:13): “North and south – [You created them; Tabor and Hermon sing forth Your name].” But see Ezekiel in prophecy that they would go out from the House of the Holy of Holies thin like the proboscides of locusts (see Tractate Pesahim, Chapter 3, Mishnah 5), and when they would arrive at this gate, they would grow strong/gather courage and become like the fulness of a small flask/jar and that is why they call them “gushing waters.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:5)


**שַׁעַר הַקָּרְבָּן.** שָׁם מַכְנִיסִים קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים שֶׁשְּׁחִיטָתָן בַּצָּפוֹן:


שער הקרבן – there they would bring in the Holy of Holies whose slaughter was in the north.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:6)


**שַׁעַר הַנָּשִׁים.** שֶׁבּוֹ הַנָּשִׁים נִכְנָסוֹת לִסְמֹךְ עַל קָרְבָּנָן לְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי יוֹסֵי דְּאָמַר נָשִׁים סוֹמְכוֹת רְשׁוּת, וּלְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן לַעֲמֹד עַל קָרְבָּנָן:


שער הנשים – for in it, the women would enter to lay their hands on their sacrifice, according to the words of Rabbi Yosi who said that women lay their hands optionally, but according to Rabbi Yehuda and Rabbi Shimon, to stand near their sacrifice (see Talmud Eruvin 96b, Rosh Hashanah 13a and Hullin 85a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:7)


**שַׁעַר הַשִּׁיר.** דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם הָיוּ מַכְנִיסִין כְּלֵי שִׁיר:


שער השיר – through that path they would bring in the musical instruments.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:8)


**שֶׁבּוֹ יָצָא יְכָנְיָהוּ בִגְלוֹתוֹ.** שֶׁנִּכְנַס לְבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת וְלִטֹּל רְשׁוּת כְּשֶׁהָלַךְ בַּגּוֹלָה לְבָבֶל, וְיָצָא דֶּרֶךְ אוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר:


שבו יצא יכניה בגלותו – when he went to the Temple to prostrate and to get permission when he went to Babylonia in the Diaspora, and he left through the path at that gate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:9)


**שַׁעַר נִיקָנוֹר.** מְפֹרָשׁ בְּיוֹמָא [לח] פֶּרֶק אָמַר לָהֶם הַמְּמֻנֶּה:


שער ניקנור – it is explained in Yoma 38a in the chapter: “The officer said to them”/אמר להם שממונה (i.e., the Prefect over the Priests).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:3:10)


**פִּשְׁפָּשִׁין.** שְׁעָרִים קְטַנִּים בְּתוֹךְ הַשְּׁעָרִים הַגְּדוֹלִים. וְאַף הֵם מִמִּנְיַן שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שְׁעָרִים. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּגַם לְשַׁעַר בֵּית הַמּוֹקֵד הָיָה לוֹ פִּשְׁפָּשׁ, לֹא חָשֵׁיב לֵיהּ, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה קָטָן בְּיוֹתֵר, אֲבָל אֵלּוּ הָיוּ גְּדוֹלִים קְצָת. וְהַךְ מַתְנִיתִין דִּשְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שְׁעָרִים מוֹקְמִינַן לַהּ בִּגְמָרָא בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי כְּאַבָּא יוֹסֵי בֶּן חָנָן. אֲבָל חֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים שִׁבְעָה שְׁעָרִים הָיוּ לָעֲזָרָה, וְאִינְהוּ סָבְרֵי דְּהָנָךְ שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה הִשְׁתַּחֲוָיוֹת כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה פְּרָצוֹת שֶׁפָּרְצוּ מַלְכֵי יָוָן בָּעֲזָרָה, וּכְשֶׁגָּבְרוּ מַלְכֵי חַשְׁמוֹנַאי וְנִצְּחוּם וְגָדְרוּ אוֹתָם פְּרָצוֹת, תִּקְנוּ שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה הִשְׁתַּחֲוָיוֹת, כְּנֶגֶד כָּל פִּרְצָה גְּדוּרָה הִשְׁתַּחֲוָיָה:


פשפשין – small gates within the larger gates and even they are from the count/number of thirteen gates, and even though the gate leading to the room (in the Temple) in which the fire is perpetually maintained (see Tractate Tamid, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1 and Tractate Middot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1, as well as Tractate Shabbat Chapter 1, Mishnah 11), also had a wicket, it was not considered because it was the smallest of them all, but the others were all slightly larger, and these of our Mishnah, of the thirteen gates, we establish in the Gemara in the Jerusalem Talmud like Abba Shaul the son of Hanan, but the Sages state that there were seven gates in the courtyard, but they hold that these thirteen prostrations correspond to the thirteen breaches made by the Grecian kings in the courtyard (see Tractate Middot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 3), and when the Hasmonean kings rose up and defeated them and repaired the breaches, they established thirteen sites of prostration corresponding to every breach that had been repaired.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:1)


**עַל שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ מְנִיחִין אֶת הָאֵבָרִים.** אַחַר הַנִּתּוּחַ מְסַדְּרִין אוֹתָן עַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן עַד שֶׁיַּעֲלוּם הַכֹּהֲנִים. וְלֹא עֲשָׂאוּם שֶׁל כֶּסֶף אוֹ שֶׁל זָהָב דְּהָא אֵין עֲנִיּוּת בִּמְקוֹם עֲשִׁירוּת, לְפִי שֶׁהַזָּהָב וְהַכֶּסֶף מַרְתִּיחִים וּמַסְרִיחִים וְהַשַּׁיִשׁ מְצַנֵּן וְשׁוֹמֵר הַבָּשָׂר שֶׁלֹּא יַסְרִיחַ, וְלֹא הָיוּ סוֹמְכִין עַל הַנֵּס שֶׁלֹּא הִסְרִיחַ בְּשַׂר הַקֹּדֶשׁ מֵעוֹלָם:


אך של שיש מניחי את האברים – after the dissection, they arrange them (i.e., the parts of the offering – the limbs) on the table until the Kohanim would bring them up, but they did not make them (i.e., the tables) of silver or gold for there is no poverty in the place of wealth, since the gold and the silver cause them (i.e., the parts of the offerings) to get hot and smell badly, whereas the marble cools them off and preserves them so that they do not smell badly, but they would not rely on a miracle that the holy meat never smelled badly (see Tractate Avot, Chapter 5, Mishnah 5, - "ולא הסריח\התליע בשר הקדש מעולם" ).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:2)


**וְעַל שֶׁל כֶּסֶף כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת.** שֶׁמוֹצִיאִין בְּכָל בֹּקֶר תִּשְׁעִים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת, כְּדִתְנָן בְּמַסֶּכֶת תָּמִיד:


ועל של כסף כלי שרת – that they would remove each morning ninety-three service vessels, as is taught in the Mishnah [of Tractate] Tamid (Chapter 3, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:3)


**נוֹתְנִים לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים בִּכְנִיסָתוֹ.** אַחַר שֶׁנֶּאֱפָה, עַד שֶׁיְּסַדְּרוּהוּ עַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן:


נותנים לחם הפנים בכניסתו – after it was baked until they would set it up on the table.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:4)


**וְעַל שֶׁל זָהָב בִּיצִיאָתוֹ.** וְהָיָה מֻנָּח שָׁם עַד זְמַן חִלּוּקוֹ:


ועל של זהב ביציאתו – and it would be placed there until the time of its division [among the priests].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:4:5)


**שֶׁמַּעֲלִין בַּקֹּדֶשׁ וְלֹא מוֹרִידִין.** דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁסִּלְּקוּהוּ מֵעַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן שֶׁל זָהָב אֵין מוֹרִידִין אוֹתוֹ לַהֲנִיחוֹ בְּשֶׁל כֶּסֶף:


שמעלין בקודש ולא מורידין – for since they removed it from on top of the golden table, and they don’t lower it to place it on one of silver.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:1)


**תְּקָלִין חַדְתִּין.** כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ בְּמַתְנִיתִין, שֶׁבּוֹ נוֹתְנִין שִׁקְלֵי שָׁנָה זוֹ, וּכְשֶׁמַּגִּיעַ זְמַן הַתְּרוּמָה, הַגִּזְבָּר מוֹצִיא כָּל שְׁקָלִים שֶׁבַּשּׁוֹפָר וְנוֹתְנָם לְלִשְׁכָּה כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְרְמוּ מֵהֶם. וְהַשֵּׁנִי כָּתוּב עָלָיו תְּקָלִין עַתִּיקִים, וּמִי שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיא שִׁקְלוֹ בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה מְבִיאוֹ בְּשָׁנָה שֶׁאַחַר כָּךְ וְנוֹתְנוֹ בְּאוֹתוֹ שׁוֹפָר, וְהַגִּזְבָּר נוֹטְלָם וְנוֹתְנָם בִּשְׁיָרֵי הַלִּשְׁכָּה. וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי כָּתוּב עָלָיו קִנִּין, וְהֵם תּוֹרִים גְּדוֹלִים. וְהָרְבִיעִי גּוֹזְלֵי עוֹלָה, בְּנֵי יוֹנָה קְטַנִּים, וְכֻלָּן עוֹלוֹת. אֲבָל הַמְּבִיאִים קִנֵּי חוֹבָה נוֹתְנִים הַמָּעוֹת אוֹ הָעוֹפוֹת לְיַד הַכֹּהֵן, וְלֹא הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים מָעוֹת לַשּׁוֹפָר. וּמְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי, מִפְּנֵי הַתַּעֲרֹבוֹת, שֶׁמָּא יָמוּת אֶחָד מִבַּעֲלֵי קִנִּין, וְנִמְצָא דְּמֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁמֵּתוּ בְּעָלֶיהָ דְּאָזְלָא לְמִיתָה מְעֹרֶבֶת בָּהֶן. אֲבָל רַבָּנָן לֹא חָיְשֵׁי לְהָכִי, וְסָבְרֵי דִּבְשׁוֹפָר שֶׁל קִנִּין מְשִׂימִין דְּמֵי קִנֵּי חוֹבָה, וּבְכָל הַמָּעוֹת הַנִּמְצָאִים בּוֹ מַקְרִיבִין קֵן אֶחָד חַטָּאת וְאֶחָד עוֹלָה, וְהַשֵּׁנִי כֻּלָּן נְדָבָה וּקְרֵבִים עוֹלוֹת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


תקלין תדתין – as it explains in on Mishnah, upon which they place the Shekaliim of this year. And when the time of Terumah offering arrives, the treasure takes out all the Shekalim that are in the horn-shaped [chest] and places them in the compartment/chamber in order that they may give Terumah from them. And the second [horn-shaped chest] has written on it “Old Shekel-dues” and whomever had not brought his Shekel in that year brings in the year after that and places it in the same horn-shaped chest, and the treasurer takes them and puts them in the remnants of the compartment/chamber. And on the third [chest] is written on it “Bird Offerings,” and they are large turtle doves. And on the fourth is written “Young Birds for Burnt Offerings,” which are small pigeons and all of them are for Burnt Offerings. But those who bring obligatory bird-offerings give the money or the birds into the hand of the Kohen, and they do not place the money in the horn-shaped chest. But the reason is explained in the Jerusalem Talmud – because of the mixing up of [the monies] lest one of the owners of the bird-offerings dies, for it is found that the waters of the sin-offering whose owners died that depart life are mixed up with them. But the Rabbis do not concern themselves with this, for they hold that in the horn-shaped [chest] for bird offerings, they place the monies of obligatory bird-offerings and with all of the monies found in it, they offer one bird sin-offering and another as a burnt offering, and the second, all of which are free-will donations and are sacrificed as burnt-offering, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:2)


**עֵצִים.** וְהַמִּתְנַדֵּב עֵצִים לַמַּעֲרָכָה נוֹתֵן דְּמֵיהֶן לְתוֹכוֹ:


עצים – A person who donates wood for the pile of wood on the altar in the Temple puts their monetary value into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:3)


**לְבוֹנָה.** הַמִּתְנַדֵּב לְבוֹנָה נוֹתֵן דָּמִים לְתוֹכוֹ, וְהַגִּזְבָּרִים לוֹקְחִים הַמָּעוֹת שֶׁבַּשּׁוֹפָר וְקוֹנִים מֵהֶן לְבוֹנָה וּמַקְטִירִים עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


לבונה – A person who donates frankincense puts monies into it and the treasurers take the monies that are in the shofar-shaped chest and purchase from them frankincense and they offer the incense on the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:4)


**זָהָב.** הַמִּתְנַדֵּב זָהָב נוֹתְנוֹ שָׁם אוֹ דְּמֵי שָׁוְיוֹ, וְהוּא לַכַּפֹּרֶת, כְּלוֹמַר לִכְלֵי שָׁרֵת. שֶׁהַמִּזְרָקוֹת נִקְרְאוּ כְּפוֹרֵי זָהָב בְּעֶזְרָא וּבְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים, לְפִי שֶׁהַכֹּהֵן מְקַנֵּחַ אֶצְבָּעוֹ בָּהֶם בֵּין הַזָּאָה לְהַזָּאָה, וּבֵין מַתָּנוֹת שֶׁל חַטָּאת, שֶׁהַשִּׁירַיִם שֶׁבָּאֶצְבַּע פְּסוּלִים:


זהב – The person who donates gold places it there, or its financial equivalent and this is for the innermost of the Temple (i.e., the Holy of Holies), that is to say, the service vessels, for the bowls out of which the sprinkling is done were called golden atonement in Ezra (1:10) and [First] Chronicles (28:17) since the Kohen would cleanse his finger with them between each sprinkling and between gifts of sin-offerings, and the left-overs that are on the finger are invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:5:5)


**שִׁשָּׁה לִנְדָבָה.** שִׁשָּׁה שׁוֹפָרוֹת הַנּוֹתָרוֹת הֵן לִנְדָבָה, הָאֶחָד כָּתוּב עָלָיו מוֹתַר חַטָּאת, הַשֵּׁנִי מוֹתַר אָשָׁם, הַשְּׁלִישִׁי מוֹתַר קִנֵּי זָבִים וְזָבוֹת וְיוֹלְדוֹת, וְהָרְבִיעִי מוֹתַר קָרְבְּנוֹת נָזִיר, הַחֲמִשִּׁי מוֹתַר אֲשַׁם מְצֹרָע, הַשִּׁשִּׁי נְדָבָה סְתָם. וּמִי שֶׁהִפְרִישׁ מָעוֹת לְחַטָּאת וְקָנָה חַטָּאתוֹ וְנִתּוֹתְרוּ מִן הַמָּעוֹת, מַשְׁלִיךְ הַמּוֹתָר לַשּׁוֹפָר שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ מוֹתַר חַטָּאת. וְכֵן מוֹתַר אָשָׁם מַשְׁלִיךְ לַשּׁוֹפָר שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ מוֹתַר אָשָׁם, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם. וְשׁוֹפָר שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ נְדָבָה סְתָם, כָּל מִי שֶׁמִּתְנַדֵּב דָּבָר לַמִּזְבֵּחַ נוֹתֵן לְתוֹכוֹ:


ששה לנדבה – the remaining six shofar-shaped [chests] are for free-will offerings. On the first, it is written on it "מותר חטאת" – the surplus monies for sin-offerings; and the second is "מותר אשם" – the surplus money for guilt offerings. The third is מותר קיני זבים וזבות ויולדות" - the surplus money for bird-offerings of men and women who had suffered a discharge and of women after childbirth. And the fourth is "מותר קרבנות נזיר" – surplus money from the offerings of a Nazirite. The fifth is מותר אשם מצורע – surplus money from guilt offering of a leper. The sixth is "נדבה סתם" – freewill offering – voluntary contributions for offerings on the Altar. But the person who separated monies for a sin offering and purchased his sin-offering and there was surplus from the monies, he casts the surplus into the shofar-shaped [chest] that the words: “surplus monies for sin-offerings” are written upon it and similarly, surplus monies from guilt-offerings he throws into the shofar-shaped [chest] on which it is written “surplus monies for guilt-offerings,” and similarly for all of them. But the shofar-shaped chest that has “freewill offerings” written up on it, anyone who wants to donate something to the altar plces it into that one.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:6:1)


**לֹא יִפְחֹת מִשְּׁנֵי גִּזְרִים.** הַמִּתְנַדֵּב עֵצִים סְתָם לֹא יִפְחוֹת מִשְּׁנֵי גִּזְרִים, כְּאוֹתָם שֶׁמְּסַדְּרִים עַל הַמַּעֲרָכָה, וְיָדוּעַ הָיָה שִׁעוּרָן. וְדַוְקָא הַמִּתְנַדֵּב עֵצִים סְתָם, אֲבָל הָרוֹצֶה לְהָבִיא אֲפִלּוּ עֵץ אֶחָד, יָבִיא:


לא יפחות מב' גזרים – a person who donates an unspecified [amount of] wood should not donate less than two logs, like those that are arranged on the pile of wood on the altar in the Temple and their measurement is known. And especially, a person who donates an unspecified [amount of] wood, but a person who desires to bring even one piece of wood should bring it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:6:2)


**לֹא יִפְחֹת מִן קֹמֶץ.** שֶׁזֶּהוּ שִׁעוּר הַלְּבוֹנָה הַבָּאָה עִם הַמִּנְחָה, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ו) וְהֵרִים מִמֶּנּוּ בְּקֻמְצוֹ מִסֹּלֶת הַמִּנְחָה וּמִשַּׁמְנָהּ וְאֶת כָּל לְבוֹנָתָהּ, מָה הֲרָמָה דְּמִנְחָה קֹמֶץ, אַף לְבוֹנָה קֹמֶץ. וְדַוְקָא הַמִּתְנַדֵּב סְתָם, אֲבָל אִם רָצָה לְהָבִיא אֲפִלּוּ קֹרְט שֶׁל לְבוֹנָה מֵבִיא. וְהַמִּתְנַדֵּב זָהָב סְתָם, לֹא יִפְחֹת מִדִּינָר זָהָב. וְהוּא שֶׁהִזְכִּיר צוּרַת מַטְבֵּעַ, אֲבָל אִם לֹא הִזְכִּיר מַטְבֵּעַ אֶלָּא זָהָב סְתָם, מֵבִיא אֲפִלּוּ צִנּוֹרָא, שֶׁהוּא כְּמִין מַזְלֵג קָטָן:


לא יפחות מן קומץ – for this is the measure of frankincense that comes with the meal-offering as it is written (Leviticus 6:8): “A handful of the choice flour and oil of the meal offering shall be taken from it, with all the frankincense that is on the meal offering, [and this token portion shall be turned into smoke on the altar as a pleasing odor to the LORD].” Just as the lifting of the meal-offering is with a handful, so also the frankincense is with a handful, but if he wants to bring even a particle/drop of frankincense, he can bring it. But a person who donates gold unspecified, should not [bring] less than a Dinar of gold, but this is when he mentioned the shape/form of the coin. But, if he did not mention a coin, but just gold, unspecified, he brings even a hook/curved pin which is a kind of small fork.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/6:6:3)
