**אָשָׁם הוּא אָשֹׁם אָשַׁם לַה'.** וְקָשֶׁה קְרָא רֵישֵׁיהּ לְסֵיפֵיהּ, דְּאָשָׁם הוּא מַשְׁמַע בַּהֲוָיָתוֹ וּבְהִלְכָתוֹ הוּא, שֶׁהוּא נֶאֱכַל לַכֹּהֲנִים, וְאָשַׁם לַה'‏ מַשְׁמַע שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ לַה'. וְדָרַשׁ יְהוֹיָדָע הַכֹּהֵן, אָשֹׁם אָשַׁם לַה', כָּל שֶׁהוּא בָּא מִשּׁוּם חֵטְא וּמִשּׁוּם אַשְׁמָה, כְּגוֹן הִפְרִישׁ מָעוֹת לְחַטָּאת וְאָשָׁם וְנִתּוֹתְרוּ מֵהֶם, יִלָּקַח בְּאוֹתוֹ הַמּוֹתַר עוֹלוֹת, הַבָּשָׂר לַה'‏ וְהָעוֹרוֹת לַכֹּהֲנִים, וְנִמְצְאוּ שְׁנֵי הַמִּקְרָאוֹת קַיָּמִים, אָשַׁם לַה'‏ הַבָּשָׂר, וְאָשָׁם לַכֹּהֲנִים הָעוֹרוֹת. וְהֵיכָן מָצִינוּ מִדְרָשׁ זֶה בִּיהוֹיָדָע, דִּכְתִיב בִּמְלָכִים (ב יב) בִּיהוֹיָדָע כֶּסֶף אָשָׁם וְכֶסֶף חַטָּאת לֹא יוּבָא בֵּית ה'‏ לַכֹּהֲנִים יִהְיוּ, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר אָשָׁם דְּכֶסֶף שֶׁקָּדַשׁ לְשֵׁם חַטָּאת וּלְשֵׁם אָשָׁם יֵהָנוּ מִמֶּנּוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים, אֶלָּא עַל כָּרְחֲךָ הָכִי קָאָמַר, יַעֲשׂוּ מִמֶּנּוּ דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּהָנוּ בּוֹ הַכֹּהֲנִים, וְהַיְנוּ עוֹלוֹת, שֶׁהָעוֹרוֹת לַכֹּהֲנִים:


אשם הוא אשום אשם לה' – but this Biblical verse (Leviticus 5:19) is difficult from its beginning to its end. The words "אשם הוא"/”it is a guilt offering implies that his existence and his going, that which is eaten is for the Kohanim and the guilt-offering is for God, implying that it is all for God. But Jehoiada the [High] Priest expounded: "אשום אשם לה'" /”he has incurred guilt before the LORD”: Everything that comes on account of a sin and on account of guilt, for example, he separated monies for a sin-offering and a guilt-offering and there was a surplus from them, he should take that surplus as burnt-offerings. “The meat is for God, and the hides go to the Kohanim and it is found that these two Biblical verses are established. He has incurred guilt before God and a guilt offering to the priests. Where do we find this Midrash concerning Jehoiada, as it is written in II Kings (12:17) regarding Jehoiada? “Money brought as a guilt offering or as a sin offering was not deposited in the House of the LORD; it went to the priests.” But it is impossible to state that the money that was sanctified for the sake of a sin-offering and for the sake of a guilt-offering that the Priests should benefit from it, but by force, this is how it should be understood: They should make of it something from it that the Kohanim will benefit from – that is burnt offerings, for the hides go to the Kohanim.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:1:1)


**מָעוֹת שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ.** קָרוֹב לַשְּׁקָלִים יִפְּלוּ לַשְּׁקָלִים. דְּבָתַר קָרוֹב אָזְלִינַן, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כא) וְהָיְתָה הָעִיר הַקְּרוֹבָה אֶל הֶחָלָל. וְהָאוֹמֵר רֹב וְקָרוֹב הֲלֵךְ אַחַר הָרֹב, מוֹקֵי מַתְנִיתִין כְּגוֹן שֶׁשְּׁקָלִים וּנְדָבָה שָׁוִין:


מעות שנמצאו. קרוב לשקלים יפלו לשקלים – for after what is near we follow, as It is written (Deuteronomy 21:3): “The elders of the town nearest to the corpse,” and the one who states majority and near, follows after the majority; our Mishnah establishes that such as a case when Shekalim and freewill donations are equivalent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:1:2)


**מֶחֱצָה עַל מֶחֱצָה יִפְּלוּ לַנְּדָבָה.** דַּחֲמִירָא מִשְּׁקָלִים, דְּכֻלָּהּ עוֹלוֹת, אֲבָל שְׁקָלִים מְבִיאִין מֵהֶן פְּעָמִים חַטָּאוֹת שֶׁנֶּאֱכָלִים לַכֹּהֲנִים. וְעוֹד, דִּשְׁיָרֵי שְׁקָלִים לְחוֹמוֹת הָעִיר וּמִגְדְּלוֹתֶיהָ:


מחצה על מחצה יפלו לנדבה – for they (i.e., freewill offerings) are more stringent than Shekalim, since they are all burnt offerings, but sometimes Shekalim are brought as sin-offerings which are consumed by Kohanim. But furthermore, the remnants of the Shekalim are used for the walls of the city and its towers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:1:3)


**מֶחֱצָה עַל מֶחֱצָה יִפְּלוּ לַלְּבוֹנָה.** דִּלְבוֹנָה הִיא עַצְמָהּ קָרְבָּן. וְעֵצִים מַכְשִׁירֵי קָרְבָּן:


מחצה על מחצה יפלו ללבונה – for frankincense is itself a sacrifice but wood is the requirement of a sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:1:4)


**מֶחֱצָה עַל מֶחֱצָה יִפְּלוּ לְגוֹזְלֵי עוֹלָה.** בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי פָּרֵיךְ, בִּשְׁלָמָא קָרוֹב לְגוֹזְלֵי עוֹלָה, כֵּיוָן דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר קוּרְבָה הֲוֵי כְּאִלּוּ וַדַּאי הֵן מִגּוֹזְלֵי עוֹלָה, אֶלָּא מֶחֱצָה עַל מֶחֱצָה, דְּמִסְּפֵיקָא שָׁדִינַן לְהוּ לְגוֹזְלֵי עוֹלָה לְחֻמְרָא, הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁהֵבִיאָה הַמָּעוֹת הַלָּלוּ בְּמַה תִּתְכַּפֵּר, שֶׁמָּא מִקִּנֵּי חוֹבָה נָפְלוּ, וּכְשֶׁאַתָּה מַקְרִיב מֵהֶן גּוֹזְלֵי עוֹלָה בְּמַה מִּתְכַּפֶּרֶת הָאִשָּׁה. וּמְשָׁנֵי, בֵּית דִּין הַמְּמֻנִּים עַל הַקִנִּין לוֹקְחִים מִשֶּׁל צִבּוּר כְּפִי אוֹתָן מָעוֹת שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ, וּמַקְנִין אוֹתָן לְבַעֲלֵי הַמָּעוֹת שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ, וּמַקְרִיבִין מֵהֶן קִנִּין עַל הַסָּפֵק, וְהַחַטָּאת לֹא תְהֵא נֶאֱכֶלֶת:


מחצה על מחצה יפלו לגוזלי עולה – In the Jerusalem Talmud (Shekalim 19a), it raises an objection: it is all right if it is closer to young pigeons for burnt offerings, since according to the Torah, we follow after that which is nearer as if they are definitively from the young pigeons for burnt offerings. But, if it is half-way between them, out of doubt, we cast them in a stringency to the young pigeons for burnt offerings, but the woman who brought these monies, with what shall she gain atonement? For perhaps, they fell from obligatory bird-offerings and when you offer from them pigeon burnt-offerings, with what shall the woman gain atonement? And it (i.e., the Jerusalem Talmud) responds that the Jewish court that is appointed over the bird-offerings takes from the community [offerings] according to those monies that were found and causes them to be acquired by the owners of the monies that were found and they offer from them bird-offerings on the doubt, and the sin-offering will not be consumed.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:2:1)


**לְעוֹלָם מַעֲשֵׂר.** אֲפִלּוּ כָּל הַשָּׁנָה כֻּלָּהּ. לְפִי שֶׁעוֹלֵי רְגָלִים אֵינָן מַסְפִּיקִין לְהוֹצִיא כָּל מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי שֶׁלָּהֶן בָּרְגָלִים, וּמְנִיחִין אוֹתָן לִקְרוֹבֵיהֶן אוֹ לְאוֹהֲבֵיהֶן לֶאֱכֹל אוֹתָן כָּל הַשָּׁנָה בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, וְעִקַּר מִצְוַת אֲכִילַת מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי הִיא שְׁלָמִים, לְפִיכָךְ רֹב בְּהֵמוֹת הַנִּמְכָּרוֹת בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם בְּכָל יְמוֹת הַשָּׁנָה הֵן נִקָּחוֹת בְּכֶסֶף מַעֲשֵׂר. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵימָר שֶׁמָּא מִן הַמּוֹכְרִים נָפְלוּ וְנִתְחַלְּלוּ כְּבָר, כֵּיוָן דְּלוֹקְחִים הֲווּ רֻבָּא, שֶׁהֲרֵי כַּמָּה אֲנָשִׁים לוֹקְחִים מִתַּגָּר אֶחָד, אָמְרִינַן דְּלוֹקְחִים הֵן וְלֹא נִתְחַלְּלוּ וַהֲרֵי הֵן מַעֲשֵׂר:


לעולם מעשר – even all of the entire year, since the pilgrims for the Festivals do not manage to spend all of their Second Tithe [monies] and leave them for their relatives or their beloved ones to consume them all year in Jerusalem. And the essential Mitzvah of consuming Second Tithe is peace-offerings. Therefore, most of the cattle that is sold in Jerusalem on all the days f the year are acquired with the money of the [Second] Tithe, and even though that one can say that perhaps they fell from the sellers and were already redeemed, since the buyers are the majority since several people purchase from one merchant, we state that they are purchases but not redeemed, and they are [Second] Tithe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:2:2)


**וּבְהַר הַבַּיִת חֻלִּין.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בָּרֶגֶל, שֶׁרֹב מָעוֹת שֶׁבְּיַד בְּנֵי אָדָם אָז הֵן שֶׁל מַעֲשֵׂר, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי הֲווּ חֻלִּין, דְּאָזְלִינַן בָּתַר רֻבָּא דְּשַׁתָּא, וְאֵמוּר מִקָּמֵי הָרֶגֶל נָפוּל:


ובהר הבית חולין – and even on the Festival (i.e., the Intermediate Days), for most of the monies in people’s hands are of [Second] Tithe which are non-holy, and we follow according to [what the case is] the majority of the year. Devoted objects/sacrifices from before the Festival, young birds are found near their nests.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:2:3)


**בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם.** שֶׁלֹּא בְּשׁוּק הַבְּהֵמוֹת:


בירושלים – that is not in the market for cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:2:4)


**בִּשְׁעַת הָרֶגֶל הַכֹּל מַעֲשֵׂר.** וְלֹא אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר רֹב שַׁתָּא, לְפִי שֶׁשְׁוָקֵי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עֲשׂוּיִים לְהִתְכַּבֵּד בְּכָל יוֹם, וְאִי נָפוּל מִקֹּדֶם כְּבָר נִמְצְאוּ. אֲבָל הַר הַבַּיִת לֹא הָיָה עָשׂוּי לְהִתְכַּבֵּד, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא גָּבוֹהַּ, וְהָרוּחַ מְכַבֵּד וּמֵסִיר כָּל הָאָבָק, וְגַם אֵין אָדָם רַשַּׁאי לִכָּנֵס בְּהַר הַבַּיִת בָּאָבָק שֶׁבְּרַגְלָיו, לְפִיכָךְ אֵין אָבָק מָצוּי שָׁם:


בשעת הרגל הכל מעשר – for we do not follow after the majority of the year because the market places/streets of Jerusalem used to be swept every day and if it would fall earlier, it is already found. But the Temple Mount was not swept because it is high and the wind sweeps and removes all the dust and also no man is permitted to enter the Temple Mount with the dust that is on his feet, therefore, there is no dust found there.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:1)


**אֵבָרִים עוֹלוֹת.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁמְּנֻתָּח כְּדֶרֶךְ הַנִּתּוּחַ הַמְפֹרָשׁ בְּעוֹלוֹת, בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁהֵן שֶׁל עוֹלוֹת:


אברים עולות – since it has been dissected in the manner of the dissection that has been explained for burnt-offerings, it is known that they are of the burnt offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:2)


**חֲתִיכוֹת חַטָּאוֹת.** דְּאֵין אוֹכְלִים בָּעֲזָרָה אֶלָּא חַטָּאוֹת וַאֲשָׁמוֹת:


חתיכות חטאות – for we consume nothing in the Temple courtyard other than sin-offerings and guilt-offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:3)


**וּבִירוּשָׁלַיִם זִבְחֵי שְׁלָמִים.** דְּרֹב בָּשָׂר הַנֶּאֱכָל בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם הֲוֵי שְׁלָמִים:


ובירושלים זבחי שלמים – for most of the meat eaten in Jerusalem is that of peace-offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:4)


**זֶה וְזֶה.** בֵּין שֶׁנִּמְצָא בָּעֲזָרָה בֵּין שֶׁנִּמְצָא בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


זה וזה – whether it is found in the Temple courtyard or whether it is found in Jerusalem.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:5)


**תְּעֻבַּר צוּרָתָן.** לְפִי שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ בְּהֶסַּח הַדַּעַת וַאֲסוּרִין בָּאֲכִילָה, וְאֵין לְזַלְזֵל בָּהֶן וּלְשָׂרְפָן עַד שֶׁיִּפָּסְלוּ בְּוַדַּאי, הִלְכָּךְ בָּעוּ עִבּוּר צוּרָה, הַיְנוּ שֶׁיִּפָּסְלוּ בְּנוֹתָר. וְעִבּוּר צוּרָה דִּשְׁלָמִים הַיְנוּ עַד יוֹם שְׁלִישִׁי:


תעובר צורתן – because they have been defiled through being given up/discarding from the mind and are prohibited for eating but we cannot despise them and burn them until they have clearly been defiled. Therefore, they require that its status must be altered/let its appearance be gone (i.e., the flesh looks disfigured by beginning to decay), which is that they be defiled through portions of sacrifices left over beyond the legal time and bound to be burned or the status must be altered for peace-offerings – which is until the third day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:6)


**נִמְצְאוּ בַגְּבוּלִים.** בְּעָרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


נמצאו בגבולים – in the cities of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:7)


**אֵבָרִים נְבֵלוֹת.** שֶׁכֵּן דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁחוֹתְכִין הַנְּבֵלוֹת לְאֵבָרִים וּמַשְׁלִיכִין בָּרְחוֹבוֹת שֶׁיֹּאכְלוּם הַכְּלָבִים:


אברים נבלות – for it is the manner that we sever the carrions (i.e., animals that died a natural death) into limbs and cast them into the streets so that the dogs would eat them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:8)


**חֲתִיכוֹת מֻתָּרִים.** שֶׁאֵין רְגִילִין לַחְתֹּךְ הַנְּבֵלוֹת לַחֲתִיכוֹת, אֶלָּא הַכְּשֵׁרוֹת רְגִילִין לַחְתֹּךְ לַחֲתִיכוֹת קְטַנּוֹת לְמָכְרָן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹ לָתֵת אוֹתָהּ בְּתוֹךְ הַקְּדֵרָה. וְדִין זֶה בְּעִיר שֶׁכֻּלָּהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל, אֲבָל בָּעִיר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ נָכְרִים, אֲפִלּוּ חֲתִיכוֹת אֲסוּרִים:


חתיכות מותרים – for it was not customary to dissect the carrions to pieces but the fit parts, it was customary to cut them into small pieces to sell them to Jews or to place it into the pot, and this law applies in a city that is completely Jewish; but in a city where there are heathens, even the pieces are forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:3:9)


**שֶׁהַבָּשָׂר מְרֻבֶּה.** וְאֵין חוֹתְכִין הַבָּשָׂר לַחֲתִיכוֹת קְטַנּוֹת אֶלָּא מְבַשְּׁלִים אוֹתוֹ אֵבָרִים:


שהבשר מרובה – we don’t dissect the flesh into small pieces but cook it by limbs.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:4:1)


**זְכָרִים עוֹלוֹת.** שֶׁאָנוּ תּוֹלִין שֶׁיָּצְאָה מִירוּשָׁלַיִם, וְרֹב זְכָרִים שֶׁבִּירוּשָׁלַיִם הֵן עוֹלוֹת, וְרֹב נְקֵבוֹת זִבְחֵי שְׁלָמִים:


זכרים עולות – for we assign that it (i.e., the animal) left from Jerusalem for most of the males that are in Jerusalem are burnt offerings, and most of the females are peace offering sacrifices.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:4:2)


**הָרָאוּי לִפְסָחִים.** זָכָר בֶּן שָׁנָה מִן הַכְּשָׂבִים אוֹ מִן הָעִזִּים:


הראוי לפסחים – a year-old male from the sheep or the goats.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:4:3)


**פְּסָחִים.** וְהַמּוֹצְאוֹ מֻתָּר לוֹ לְהַקְרִיבוֹ לְפִסְחוֹ, וְאִם יָבֹא בְּעָלָיו אַחַר כָּךְ יִתֵּן לוֹ אֶת דָּמָיו:


פסחים – and he who finds it is permitted to him to offer it as his Passover offering. But if its owner comes afterwards, he should give him its monetary value.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:4:4)


**קֹדֶם לָרֶגֶל שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם.** מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁמַּתְחִילִין לִדְרֹשׁ בְּהִלְכוֹת הַפֶּסַח וְאָדָם מַפְרִישׁ אֶת פִּסְחוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


קודם לרגל שלשים יום – from the time that they begin to expound upon the laws of Passover, and a person separates his Passover-offering. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:5:1)


**הָיוּ מְמַשְׁכְּנִין אֶת מוֹצְאֶיהָ.** הַמּוֹצֵא עוֹלָה אוֹ שְׁלָמִים הָיוּ מְמַשְׁכְּנִין אוֹתוֹ עַד שֶׁיָּבִיא נְסָכֶיהָ מִשֶּׁלּוֹ, שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים לַפָּר וּשְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים לָאַיִל וְעִשָּׂרוֹן לַכֶּבֶשׂ:


היו ממשכנין את מוצאיה – a person who would find a burnt offering or a peace offering, they would exact a pledge from him until he would bring its libations from his own – three-tenths of an Ephah for a bull and two-tenths of an Ephah for a ram and one-tenth for a lamb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:5:2)


**מַנִּיחִין אוֹתָהּ וּבוֹרְחִים.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא לְהִתְחַיֵּב בִּנְסָכֶיהָ:


מניחין אותה ובורחים – in order that he would not be liable for its libations.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:1)


**שִׁבְעָה דְבָרִים.** וְקָחָשֵׁיב לְהוּ וְאָזֵיל:


שבעה דברים – that he considered for them and continuing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:2)


**נָכְרִי שֶׁשָּׁלַח עוֹלָתוֹ.** דְּדָרְשִׁינַן אִישׁ אִישׁ, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁהַנָּכְרִים נוֹדְרִים נְדָרִים וּנְדָבוֹת כְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


נכרי ששלח עולתו – for we expound [the words] "איש איש"/”every person” which teaches that heathens make votive offerings and freewill offerings like Jews do.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:3)


**שֶׁתְּהֵא מִנְחָתוֹ.** עֲשִׂירִית הָאֵיפָה שֶׁכֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל מַקְרִיב בְּכָל יוֹם מַחֲצִיתָהּ בַּבֹּקֶר וּמַחֲצִיתָהּ בָּעֶרֶב:


שתהא מנחתו – one-tenth of an Ephah that the High Priest would offer each day, one-half of it in the morning and one-half of in the evening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:4)


**קְרֵבָה מִשֶּׁל צִבּוּר.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ו) חָק עוֹלָם, חֹק זֶה יִהְיֶה מִשֶּׁל עוֹלָם, כְּלוֹמַר מִשֶּׁל צִבּוּר, דְּהַיְנוּ מִתְּרוּמַת הַלִּשְׁכָּה:


קריבה משל צבור – as it is written (Leviticus 6:11): [“Only the males among Aaron’s descendants may eat of it, as their due] for all time [throughout the ages from the LORD’s offerings by fire].” This law will be for eternity, meaning to say, from the community, from the sacred donations of the chamber.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר מִשֶּׁל יוֹרְשִׁים.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְהַכֹּהֵן הַמָּשִׁיחַ תַּחְתָּיו מִבָּנָיו, וְהָכִי מַשְׁמַע, וְהַכֹּהֵן הַמָּשִׁיחַ שֶׁמֵּת, תַּחְתָּיו אֶחָד מִבָּנָיו יַעֲשֶׂה אוֹתָהּ:


רבי יהודה אומר משל יורשים – as it is written (Leviticus 6:15): “And so shall the priest, anointed from among his son to succeed him, [shall prepare it; it is the LORD’s – a law for all time – to be turned entirely into smoke],” and this implies that the Kohen that was anointed who died, succeeding him, one of his sons shall prepare it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:6:6)


**וּשְׁלֵמָה הָיְתָה קְרֵבָה.** כְּשֶׁהִיא בָּאָה מִשֶּׁל צִבּוּר לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹ מִשֶּׁל יוֹרְשִׁים לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, עִשָּׂרוֹן שָׁלֵם הָיְתָה קְרֵבָה וְלֹא חֲצִי עִשָּׂרוֹן. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַפִּיק לֵיהּ מִדִּכְתִיב מִבָּנָיו יַעֲשֶׂה אוֹתָהּ, כְּשֶׁהָאֶחָד מִבָּנָיו מַקְרִיב לְאַחַר שֶׁמֵּת אָבִיו, דְּהַיְנוּ הַיּוֹרְשִׁים, יַעֲשֶׂה אוֹתָהּ וְלֹא חֶצְיָהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יִהוּדָה, שֶׁמִּשֶּׁל יוֹרְשִׁים הִיא בָּאָה. וְהָא דִּתְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין דִּתְנַאי בֵּית דִּין הוּא לְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שֶׁתְּהֵא בָּאָה מִן הַצִּבּוּר, וְלֹא מִן הַתּוֹרָה, מְפָרֵשׁ בִּגְמָרָא דִּשְׁתֵּי תַּקָּנוֹת הֲווּ, בִּתְחִלָּה מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא קְרֵבָה מִשֶּׁל צִבּוּר, מִדִּכְתִיב חָק עוֹלָם כִּדְאָמְרִינַן, כֵּיוָן שֶׁרָאוּ דְּקָא מִדַּחֲקָא לִשְׁכָּה, הִתְקִינוּ דְּנִגְבּוּ מִיּוֹרְשִׁים, כֵּיוָן דְּחָזוּ דְּקָא פָּשְׁעוּ בָּהּ, אוֹקְמוּהָ אַדְּאוֹרַיְתָא. וְנִמְצֵאת עַכְשָׁיו קְרֵבָה מִן הַצִּבּוּר בִּתְנַאי בֵּית דִּין, דְּאוּקְמוּהָ אַדְּאוֹרַיְתָא:


ושלימה היתה קריבה – when it comes from the community, according to Rabbi Shimon, or from the heirs, according to Rabbi Yehuda. A full tenth was offered and not a one-half tenth. Rabbi Shimon derives it from "כליל תקטר"/”to be turned entirely into smoke” (Leviticus 6:15), that he should not offer incense in halves but whole, when it comes from the community. But Rabbi Yehuda derives it as it is written (Leviticus 6:15): “from among his son to succeed him, prepare it,” that one of his sons will offer incense after his father dies, that is, the heirs will prepare it and not half of it, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda that it comes from the heirs. But that our Mishnah states that the Jewish court instituted a condition, according to the words of Rabbi Shimon that it should come from the community. But it is not from the Torah – the Gemara (Jerusalem Talmud Shekalim 20b) explains that there were two enactments at first that from the Torah it was offered from the community, since it was written “ a law for all time” (Leviticus 6:15) as we have stated. Since we saw that the chamber was hurried, they enacted that it would be collected from the heirs. Since they saw that they were negligent with it, they established to be from the Torah, and it was found now to be offered from the community with the condition of the Jewish court that it would be established by the Torah.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:7:1)


**שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים נֵאוֹתִים בָּהֶן.** דַּוְקָא לַאֲכִילַת הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת. אֲבָל לַאֲכִילַת חֻלִּין, אֲפִלּוּ לִמְלֹחַ חֻלִּין הַנֶּאֱכָלִין בַּעֲזָרָה עִם הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהוּ הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת נֶאֱכָלִים עַל הַשּׂבַע, אֵין נֵאוֹתִים בָּהֶן:


שיהיו הכהנים נאותין בהן – specifically regarding the consuming of the sacrifices. But, regarding the eating of non-holy things, even the salting of non-holy things that are eaten in the Temple courtyard with the sacrifices in order that the sacrifices would be eaten with satiation, they don’t enjoy them (i.e., the salt and the wood).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:7:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ מוֹעֲלִין בְּאֶפְרָהּ.** דְּמִן הַתּוֹרָה אֵין מוֹעֲלִין בְּאֶפְרָהּ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) חַטָּאת הִיא, בָּהּ מוֹעֲלִין וְאֵין מוֹעֲלִין בְּאֶפְרָהּ. כֵּיוָן דְּחָזוּ דְּקָא מְזַלְזְלֵי בָּהּ, גָּזְרוּ בְּאֶפְרָהּ מְעִילָה. כֵּיוָן דְּחָזוּ דְּקָא פָּרְשֵׁי [מֵאֵפֶר] הַזָּאוֹת, אוֹקְמוּהָ אַדְּאוֹרַיְתָא:


שלא היו מועלין באפרה – for from the Torah, they cannot commit religious sacrilege with its ashes, as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “[A man who is clean shall gather up the ashes of the ow and deposit them outside the camp in a clean place, to be kept for water of lustration for the Israelite community.] It is for cleansing.” Through it they commit religious sacrilege but they don’t commit religious sacrilege through its ashes, but since they saw that they were despising it, they decreed that through its ashes is religious sacrilege because they saw that they separated themselves from the doubt of sprinklings, they established it as from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:7:3)


**וְעַל הַקִּנִּין הַפְּסוּלוֹת.** הַמְּחֻיָּבִין קִנִּין מְבִיאִין מָעוֹת וְנוֹתְנִים לַשּׁוֹפָר, וּבֵית דִּין לוֹקְחִין הַמָּעוֹת וְקוֹנִים בָּהֵן קִנִּין, וְהַבְּעָלִים הוֹלְכִים לָהֶם וְסוֹמְכִים עַל בֵּית דִּין שֶׁיַּקְרִיבוּ קִנֵּיהֶן, וְאִם פָּרְחוּ הַקִנִּין אוֹ נִמְצְאוּ פְּסוּלִים, תְּנַאי בֵּית דִּין הוּא שֶׁיִּקְּחוּ אֲחֵרִים מִדְּמֵי הַלִּשְׁכָּה, וּמַקְנִים אוֹתָם לַבְּעָלִים, וְיוֹצְאִין בָּהֶן יְדֵי חוֹבָתָן:


ועל הקינין הפסולות – those obligated for bird-offerings they bring the money and place it into shofar-shaped chest and the Jewish court takes the money and purchases with them the bird-offerings and the owners go to them and rely upon the Jewish court that they will offer their bird-offerings but if the birds flew off or were found to be invalid, it is a condition of the Jewish court that they would take others from the monies of the chamber and give possession to the owners and fulfill through the their religious obligation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/7:7:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר.** אוֹתוֹ שֶׁהוּא רָגִיל לְסַפֵּק אֶת הַקִּנִּין, שֶׁפָּסַק דָּמִים עִם הַגִּזְבָּרִים לִמְכֹּר לָהֶם כָּל הַקִנִּין הַצְּרִיכִים, הוּא חַיָּב לְהַחְלִיף כָּל שֶׁיִּמָּצֵא בָּהֶן פְּסוּל, כְּדִתְנַן לְעֵיל פֶּרֶק הַתְּרוּמָה, אֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל מְעוֹתָיו עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מְרַצֶּה, וְאִם הֶחְמִיץ הַיַּיִן אוֹ הִתְלִיעַ הַסֹּלֶת מַחֲזִירִין אוֹתוֹ לַמּוֹכֵר, כָּךְ אִם נִפְסַל הָעוֹף אַחַר לְקִיחָתוֹ מַחֲזִירוֹ לוֹ, וְהוּא מַחֲזִיר אֶת הַדָּמִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי, דְּהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי, תְּנַאי בֵּית דִּין הוּא, הַמְּסַפֵּק אֶת הַקִּנִּין מְסַפֵּק אֶת הָאוֹבְדוֹת וְאֶת הַפְּסוּלוֹת:


רבי יוסי אומר – the one who regularly supplied the bird-offerings who fixed the price with the treasurers to sell them all the bird sacrifices that are needed, he is obligated to exchange all that is found in them something invalid, as is taught in the Mishnah (nine) above in the chapter “The Sacred Contributions”/"התרומה", he doesn’t receive his funds until the Altar is satisfied. But if the wine fermented or the fine flour became wormy, they return it to him and he returns the money, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi, for this is what we say in the Jerusalem Talmud that it is a condition of the Jewish court that whomever supplies the bird offerings supplies that which becomes lost or that is invalid.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:1:1)


**כָּל הָרֻקִּין הַנִּמְצְאִים בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם טְהוֹרִים.** וְלֹא מַחֲזִיקִינַן לְהוּ בְּרֻקּוֹ שֶׁל זָב וְזָבָה שֶׁהֵן מְטַמְּאִין אָדָם וְכֵלִים, מִשּׁוּם דְּאָזְלִינַן בָּתַר רֻבָּהּ:


כל הרוקין הנמצאים בירושלם אהורים – and we do not encourage the spittle of a man or woman suffering from an emission because they defile humans and utensils because we follow after the majority.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:1:2)


**חוּץ מִשֶּׁל שׁוּק הָעֶלְיוֹן.** לְפִי שֶׁכּוֹבְסִין נָכְרִים הָיוּ מְצוּיִין שָׁם, וַחֲכָמִים גָּזְרוּ עַל הַנָּכְרִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כְּזָבִין לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶן וְרֻקָּן טָמֵא. וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי, לְפִי שֶׁשּׁוּק הָעֶלְיוֹן לֹא הָיְתָה דְּרִיסַת הָרֶגֶל מְצוּיָה שָׁם, וְזָבִין וְזָבוֹת שֶׁמִּתְבּוֹדְדִין וּמִתְרַחֲקִין מִבְּנֵי אָדָם הָיוּ מְצוּיִין שָׁם:


חוץ משל שוק העליון – because heathen launderers were found there and the Sages decreed concerning the heathens that they would be like men with a flux in all their matters, and their spittle is ritually impure. But there are those who state that that since the Upper Market did not have the benefit of the crossing found there (i.e., “foot traffic”) and men and women with a flux were alone and distanced themselves from other people that were found there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:1:3)


**שֶׁבָּאֶמְצַע טְמֵאִים.** מִשּׁוּם דְּזָבִין וְזָבוֹת שְׁכִיחֵי וּמְהַלְּכִים בְּאֶמְצַע דְּרָכִים, וְהַטְּהוֹרִים מִסְתַּלְּקִים לְצִדֵּי הַדְּרָכִים, מְקוֹם שֶׁאֵין דְּרִיסַת הָרֶגֶל מְצוּיָה, וּמַזְהִירִין לְכָל טָמֵא שֶׁלֹּא יִגַּע בָּהֶן. אֲבָל בִּשְׁעַת הָרֶגֶל, שֶׁרֹב יִשְׂרָאֵל טְהוֹרִין, הַטְּמֵאִים מִסְתַּלְּקִים לַצְּדָדִים שֶׁלֹּא יְטַמְּאוּ אֶת הָעָם, וְהַטְּהוֹרִים הוֹלְכִים בְּאֶמְצַע הַדְּרָכִים, הִלְכָּךְ בִּשְׁעַת הָרֶגֶל רֻקִּין הַנִּמְצָאִים בָּאֶמְצַע טְהוֹרִים וְשֶׁבַּצְּדָדִים טְמֵאִין. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


שבאמצע טמאים – because men and women with a flux are frequently there and walk in the middle of the roads, and the ritually pure individuals remove themselves to the sides of the roads – in a place where there is not much walking found, and are careful of all ritually impure that they should not come in contact with them, but at the time of the Festival, when the majority of Jews are ritually pure, the ritually impure remove themselves to the sides so that they do not defile the people, and the ritually pure walk in the middle of the roads; therefore, at the time of the Festival, the spittle that is found in the middle of the road is ritually pure, but that which is on the sides is ritually impure, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ יְרִידָה לְבֵית הַטְּבִילָה.** שְׁנֵי דְּרָכִים הָיוּ לַמִּקְוֶה, בְּאֶחָד יוֹרְדִים וּבְאֶחָד עוֹלִים, שֶׁלֹּא יִגְּעוּ הַטְּמֵאִים בַּטְּהוֹרִים. וְשֶׁל דֶּרֶךְ יְרִידָה וַדַּאי טְמֵאִים, לְפִי שֶׁנָּפְלוּ מִיַּד הַמְּבִיאָם לַטְּבִילָה:


דרך ירידה לבית הטבילה – there were two paths to the ritual path – on one, they go down and on the other they go up – so that the ritually impure do not come in contact with the ritually pure, and that of the path going down is certainly impure, because they fell from the hand of those bringing them for ritual immersion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא כִּירִידָתָן עֲלִיָּתָן.** דְּבִירִידָתָן וַדַּאי טְמֵאִים הָיוּ, לְכָךְ הָיָה מוֹרִידָן לְבֵית הַטְּבִילָה, וּבַעֲלִיָּתָן טָהֲרוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁהִטְבִּילָן:


שלא כירידתן עלייתן – for one their descent, they were definitely ritually impure; therefore, they would bring them down to the place of ritual immersion and upon their ascent they were ritually pure after they had been immersed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:3)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר כֻּלָּן טְהוֹרִין.** דְּלֹא גָּזְרוּ עַל סְפֵק כֵּלִים בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר כולן טהורין – they did not make the decree on doubtful utensils in Jerusalem and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:4)


**מַגְרֵפָה.** כְּלִי שֶׁגּוֹרְפִים וְכוֹנְסִים בּוֹ עַצְמוֹת הַמֵּתִים כְּשֶׁהֵם מְפֻזָּרִים:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:2:5)


**מְרִצָּה.** כְּלִי שֶׁרוֹצְצִים וּמְשַׁבְּרִים בּוֹ עַצְמוֹת הַמֵּת כְּשֶׁרוֹצִים לְהַכְנִיסָם בַּסַּל לְהוֹלִיכָן מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם:


מריצה – a tool that crushes and breaks in it the bones of the dead when they want to bring them in a basket to take them from place to place.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:1)


**שׁוֹחֵט בָּהּ מִיָּד.** דְּוַדַּאי הִטְבִּילוּהָ בְּעָלֶיהָ מֵאֶתְמוֹל כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לָהּ הַעֲרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ וּתְהֵא רְאוּיָה לִשְׁחֹט בָּהּ הַיּוֹם בְּעֶרֶב הַפֶּסַח:


שוחט בה מיד – for certainly, its owner immersed it [in a ritual bath] from yesterday in order that it would have a sunset (required after purification, to be deemed entirely clean) and that it would be appropriate to slaughter with it on the day of the Eve of Passover.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:2)


**בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר שׁוֹנֶה וּמַטְבִּיל.** כְּלוֹמַר אַף עַל גַּב דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵימָר כְּבָר הִטְבִּילוּהָ בְּעָלֶיהָ, צָרִיךְ הַמּוֹצְאָהּ לְהַטְבִּילָהּ שֵׁנִית, דְּשֶׁמָּא לֹא הִטְבִּילוּהָ בְּעָלֶיהָ, דְּהָיָה לָהֶם שְׁהוּת לְהַמְתִּין עַד סָמוּךְ לַחֲשֵׁכָה, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא גָּזְרוּ עַל סְפֵק כֵּלִים בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, מִשּׁוּם חֻמְרָא דְּקָדָשִׁים הִצְרִיכוּהָ טְבִילָה. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ שׁוֹנֶה לְהַזּוֹת עָלֶיהָ, דְּשֶׁמָּא נִטְמְאָה בְּמֵת וְהִזָּה עָלֶיהָ בַּשְּׁלִישִׁי, וְעַתָּה שׁוֹנֶה וּמַזֶּה עָלֶיהָ וְאַחַר כָּךְ מַטְבִּילָהּ:


בשלשה עשר שונה ומטביל – meaning to say, even though it is possible to state that its owner already immersed it [in a ritual bath], the person who finds it must immerse it a second time for perhaps, its owner had not immersed it and they had a while to wait until it was close to darkness, and even though they did not decree on doubtful utensils in Jerusalem because the stringency of holy objects required immersion for it. But Maimonides explained [the word] "שונה" /”a second time,” to mean to sprinkle upon it for it had become ritually impure through contact with the dead, and he sprinkled [from the ashes of the red heifer] on the third day (see Numbers 19:12), and now sprinkles once again upon it and afterwards immerses it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:3)


**קוֹפִיץ.** סַכִּין גָּדוֹל עָשׂוּי לַחְתֹּךְ בָּשָׂר וְלִשְׁבֹּר בּוֹ עֲצָמוֹת:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:4)


**בָּזֶה וּבָזֶה.** בֵּין בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר בֵּין בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר:


בזה ובזה – whether on the thirteenth or the fourteenth [of Nisan].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:5)


**שׁוֹנֶה וּמַטְבִּיל.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין הַקּוֹפִיץ רָאוּי אֶלָּא לַחֲגִיגָה, שֶׁשְּׁבִירַת עֶצֶם מֻתָּר בָּהּ. וּבִגְמָרָא בִּפְסָחִים (דף ע) מוֹקֵי מַתְנִיתִין בִּזְמַן שֶׁהָיָה הַנָּשִׂיא גּוֹסֵס בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר, שֶׁאִם יָמוּת יִהְיוּ כָּל הַצִּבּוּר טְמֵאִים וְיֻקְרַב הַפֶּסַח בְּטֻמְאָה וְלֹא יִהְיוּ צְרִיכִים לְטַהֵר הַכֵּלִים. הִלְכָּךְ, סַכִּין שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִשְׁחִיטַת הַפֶּסַח, דְּחַד סְפֵקָא הוּא, שֶׁמָּא לֹא יָמוּת הַנָּשִׂיא וְיַעֲשׂוּ אֶת הַפֶּסַח בְּטָהֳרָה, מַטְבִּילִין אוֹתוֹ, וַחֲזָקָה שֶׁטִּהֲרוּהוּ בְּיוֹם שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר כְּדֵי לִשְׁחֹט בּוֹ אֶת הַפֶּסַח בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר. אֲבָל קוֹפִיץ, דִּתְרֵי סְפֵקֵי, שֶׁמָּא יָמוּת הַנָּשִׂיא וְיַעֲשׂוּ פֶּסַח בְּטֻמְאָה וְאָז אֵין מְבִיאִין חֲגִיגָה וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לַקּוֹפִיץ, וְאַף אִם לֹא יָמוּת שֶׁמָּא יִהְיוּ הַפְּסָחִים מְרֻבִּים וְלֹא יַעֲשׂוּ חֲגִיגָה בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא מַטְבְּלֵי לֵיהּ וְחֶזְקָתוֹ שֶׁלֹּא טִהֲרוּהוּ:


שונה ומטביל – he immerses it again for the hatchet/curved cutting tool is not appropriate other than for the Hagigah/the Festival Offering for the breaking of a bone is permitted on it, and in the Gemara of Pesahim (Tractate Pesahim 70b) establishes that at the time when the Nasi/head of the Sanhedrin is on his deathbed on the thirteenth [of Nisan], for if he would die, the entire community will be ritually impure and will sacrifice the Passover offering in defilement, but they would not to ritually purify the utensils; therefore, the knife that one needs for the slaughtering of the Passover offering – this is one doubt, for perhaps the Nasi won’t died and they will make the Passover offering in ritual purity, they immerse it and there is a presumption that they purified it on the thirteenth day in order to slaughter the Passover offering on the fourteenth. Bu the hatchet/curved cutting tool, there are two doubts: a) perhaps the Nasi will die and they will make the Passover offering in a state of ritual defilement and they don’t bring the Hagigah/Festival offering, and there is no need for the hatchet/curved cutting tool, and even if he doesn’t die, perhaps the Passover offerings are many and they don’t do the Hagigah/Festival Offering on the fourteenth; therefore, they don’t immerse it and b) it has the presumption that it they did not purify it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:6)


**חָל אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר לִהְיוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת שׁוֹחֵט בָּהּ מִיָּד.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן כֵּיוָן דְּשַׁבָּת הוּא לֹא נִשְׁחֹט בָּהּ מִסְּפֵיקָא שֶׁמָּא טְמֵאָה הִיא וְנִמְצָא הַפֶּסַח טָמֵא וּמְחַלֵּל אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת שֶׁלֹּא בִּמְקוֹם מִצְוָה. וְרַבּוֹתַי פֵּרְשׁוּ, שׁוֹחֵט בָּהּ מִיָּד, בַּקּוֹפִיץ קָאָמַר, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין חֲגִיגָה בְּשַׁבָּת, מִכָּל מָקוֹם חֲזָקָה שֶׁהִטְבִּילָהּ מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת לְצֹרֶךְ חֲגִיגַת חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר:


חל י"ד להיות בשבת שוחט בה מיד – but we don’t say that since it is Shabbat, we will not slaughter it out of a doubt that perhaps it was ritually impure and it is found that the Passover offering was ritually impure, and we desecrate the Sabbath not at the place of a commandment. But my teachers explained that we slaughter it immediately with a hatchet/curved cutting tool stating that even though there is no Festival offering/Hagigah on the Sabbath, nevertheless, there is a presumption that he immersed it from the Eve of the Sabbath (i.e., Friday) for the need of the Festival offering/Hagigah on the fifteenth [of Nisan].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:7)


**בַּחֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר שׁוֹחֵט בָּהּ מִיָּד.** לְפִי שֶׁכָּל הָעָם יוֹדְעִים שֶׁהַזָּאָה וּטְבִילָה אֲסוּרָה בְּיוֹם טוֹב, וּמַטְבִּילִין אֶת הַכֹּל מִלִּפְנֵי יוֹם טוֹב:


בחמשה עשר שוחט בה מיד – because the entire people knows that sprinkling and immersion are prohibited on the Jewish festival/Yom Tov, and we immerse everything from before the Jewish festival.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:8)


**נִמְצֵאת.** הַקּוֹפִיץ:


נמצאת – the hatchet/curved cutting tool


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:3:9)


**קְשׁוּרָה לְסַכִּין.** אָמְרִינַן וַדַּאי הִטְבִּילוּהָ עִם הַסַּכִּין. אֲבָל רַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, נִמְצֵאת סַכִּין סְתָמָא קְשׁוּרָה עִם סַכִּין אַחֶרֶת יְדוּעָה, הֲרֵי הִיא כַּסַּכִּין הַיְּדוּעָה, אִם טְמֵאָה טְמֵאָה וְאִם טְהוֹרָה טְהוֹרָה:


קשורה לסכין – is tied to the knife. We stated that it was definitely immersed with the knife, but Maimonides explained that it is found that an undefined knife is tied with another known knife, it is like the known knife; if it (i.e., the known knife) is ritual impure, it (i.e., the other knife) is ritually impure; if it (i.e., the known knife) is ritually pure, it (i.e., the other knife) is ritually pure.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:1)


**פָּרֹכֶת שֶׁנִּטְמֵאת בִּוְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה.** שֶׁנָּגְעָה בְּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים, שֶׁחֲכָמִים גָּזְרוּ עַל הַמַּשְׁקִין שֶׁמְּטַמְּאִין כֵּלִים, גְּזֵרָה מִשּׁוּם מַשְׁקֶה דְּזָב וְזָבָה כְּגוֹן רֻקּוֹ וְשִׁכְבַת זַרְעוֹ שֶׁהֵם אַב הַטֻּמְאָה וּמְטַמְּאִין אָדָם וְכֵלִים מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא:


פרוכת שנטמת בולד הטומאה – [the Curtain] that touched ritually impure liquids that the Sages decreed regarding these liquids that they defile utensils, as a decree because of the liquid of a man or woman with a flux, such as his spittle or nocturnal emission, which are one of the original or direct causes of Levitical uncleanness, and from the Torah, they defile humans and utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:2)


**מַטְבִּילִין אוֹתָהּ בִּפְנִים.** וְאֵינָהּ טְעוּנָה שִׁלּוּחַ מִחוּץ לְמַחֲנֶה שְׁכִינָה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא נִטְמֵאת אֶלָּא בִּוְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה:


מטבילין אותה בפנים – and it does not require being sent outside of the camp of the Divine Majesty (from the entrance of the court and further), because it is was not defiled other than by a secondary cause of Levitical uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:3)


**וּמַכְנִיסִין אוֹתָהּ מִיָּד.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ טְעוּנָה הַעֲרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ:


ומכניסין אותה מיד – and it does not require having a sunset (after immersion) for complete purification.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:4)


**נִטְמֵאת בְּאַב הַטֻּמְאָה.** בְּשֶׁרֶץ אוֹ בִּנְבֵלָה:


נטמאת באב הטומאה – with an unclean reptile or a carrion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:5)


**מַטְבִּילִין אוֹתָהּ בַּחוּץ.** דִּטְעוּנָה שִׁלּוּחַ, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק בַּתְרָא דְּעֵרוּבִין, הַמַּכְנִיס טְמֵא שֶׁרֶץ בְּמִקְדָּשׁ חַיָּב:


מטבילין אותה בחוץ – we immerse it outside for it requires being sent out [of the camp] as we state in the last chapter of Tractate Eruvin (Chapter 10, Mishnah 15; Talmud Eruvin 104b), that a person who brings in an unclean reptile in the Temple is liable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:4:6)


**וְשׁוֹטְחִין אוֹתָהּ בַּחֵיל.** חוּץ לַעֲזָרָה:



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:1)


**נִימִין.** ליצי"ש בְּלַעַ"ז:


נימין – “Lytzish” in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:2)


**עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה חוּטִין.** לְפִי שֶׁהִיא עֲשׂוּיָה מִתְּכֵלֶת וְאַרְגָּמָן וְתוֹלַעַת שָׁנִי וָשֵׁשׁ, וְכָל מִין וּמִין חוּטוֹ כָּפוּל שֵׁשׁ. כִּדְאִיתָא בְּיוֹמָא:


עשרים וארבעה חוטין – because it is made from blue, purple and crimson yarns and fine linen and each and every species, its thread is doubled six times as is mentioned in Yoma (Talmud Yoma 87a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:3)


**אָרְכָּהּ אַרְבָּעִים וְרָחְבָּהּ עֶשְׂרִים.** כְּמִדַּת פֶּתַח הָאוּלָם, שֶׁהָיָה גָּבְהוֹ אַרְבָּעִים וְרָחְבּוֹ עֶשְׂרִים:


ארכה מ' ורחבה כ' – like the measure of the hall leading to the interior of the Temple (see Tractate Middot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 7), that its height (it should be “length,” based upon the commentary of the Tosafot Yom Tov) was forty cubits and its width was twenty cubits (see also Tractate Middot, Chapter 3, Mishnah 6; but we also learn in Chapter 3, Mishnah 7 that the height was also forty cubits).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:4)


**וּמִשְּׁמֹנִים וּשְׁתַּיִם רִבּוֹא.** כָּךְ הָיָה מִנְיַן הַחוּטִים שֶׁהִיא נַעֲשֵׂית מֵהֶן. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, מִנְיַן דִּינְרֵי זָהָב שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹצִיאִין עָלֶיהָ. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁכָּתוּב בָּהֶן וּבִשְׁמוֹנִים וּשְׁתַּיִם רִיבוֹת, כְּלוֹמַר דִּשְׁמוֹנִים וּשְׁתַּיִם נְעָרוֹת בְּתוּלוֹת הָיוּ מִתְעַסְּקוֹת בַּעֲשִׂיָּתָהּ:


ושמונים ושתים רבוא – this was the number of threads from which it was made. Another explanation: the number of gold dinars that they spent on it. But there are books in which it is written in them that “that with twenty thousand and eighty,” meaning to say that there eighty-two young virgins were engaged in making it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:5:5)


**וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת כֹּהֲנִים הָיוּ מַטְבִּילִין אוֹתָהּ.** דְּכֵלִים הַנִּגְמָרִים אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּגְמְרוּ בְּטָהֳרָה צְרִיכִים טְבִילָה לְקֹדֶשׁ, כִּדְאִיתָא בְּמַסֶּכֶת חֲגִיגָה. וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת כֹּהֲנִים דִּתְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין לְשׁוֹן גֻּזְמָא וַהֲבַאי הוּא, דְּלֹא הָיָה צָרִיךְ כָּל כָּךְ כֹּהֲנִים לְהַטְבִּילָהּ:


ושלש מאות כהנים היו מטבילין אותה – utensils that were completed even though they were completed in ritual purity, require immersion for holy things, as is found in Tractate Hagigah (Chapter 3, Mishnah 2). The three hundred Kohanim that are taught in our Mishnah is the language of hyperbole and exaggeration, for they did not require so many Kohanim to immerse it.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:6:1)


**הַכֹּל יִשָּׂרֵף בִּפְנִים.** בְּבֵית הַדֶּשֶׁן הַגָּדוֹל שֶׁהָיָה בַּעֲזָרָה, שֶׁשָּׁם שׂוֹרְפִים פְּסוּלֵי קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים, שֶׁכָּל שֶׁפְּסוּלוֹ בַּקֹּדֶשׁ שׂוֹרְפִין אוֹתוֹ בַּקֹּדֶשׁ:


הכל ישרף בפנים – in the large place where the ashes of sacrifices were deposited which was in the Temple courtyard for there they would burn the unfit/disqualified meat of the most holy sacrifices for everything that was disqualified/unfit for the holy sacrifice, we burn it in the holy [precincts].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:6:2)


**חוּץ מִשֶּׁנִּטְמָא בְּאַב הַטֻּמְאָה בַּחוּץ.** דְּכֵיוָן דַּחֲמִירָא טֻמְאָתוֹ וְאֵרַע פְּסוּלוֹ בַּחוּץ, אֵין לְהַכְנִיסוֹ לַעֲזָרָה לְשָׂרְפוֹ, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא הָיָה פְּסוּלוֹ בַּקֹּדֶשׁ:


חוץ מנטמא באב הטומאה בחוץ – for since its defilement is stringent and its disqualification occurred outside, one cannot bring it into the Temple courtyard to burn it for its disqualification did not occur in the holy [precincts].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:6:3)


**חוּץ מִשֶּׁנִּטְמָא בִּוְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה בִּפְנִים.** דְּאִית בֵּיהּ תַּרְתֵּי, טֻמְאָתוֹ בִּפְנִים וְקִילָא טֻמְאָתוֹ. אֲבָל נִטְמָא בְּאַב הַטֻּמְאָה אֵין לְהַשְׁהוֹת הַטֻּמְאָה בִּפְנִים עַד שֶׁתִּשָּׂרֵף, אֶלָּא יוֹצִיאֶנָּה מִיָּד וְיִשְׂרְפֶנָּה בַּחוּץ:


חוץ משנטמא בולד הטומאה בפנים – for it has two [leniencies]: its defilement was inside, and the leniency of its ritual defilement, but if it was defiled by a primary uncleanness, we do not cause the delay of the ritual defilement inside until it is burned but we remove it immediately and burn it outside.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:7:1)


**וְאֶת שֶׁנִּטְמָא בִּוְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה בֵּין בַּחוּץ בֵּין בִּפְנִים יִשָּׂרֵף בִּפְנִים.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּטָהוֹר מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא, אֲפִלּוּ נִטְמָא בַּחוּץ יֵשׁ לְקַיֵּם בּוֹ שְׂרֵפָתוֹ בַּקֹּדֶשׁ:


את שנטמא בולד הטומאה בין בפנים בין בחוץ ישרף בפנים – since it is ritually pure from the Torah, even if it became defiled outside, we must fulfill its burning in the holy [precincts].



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:1)


**מֵחֲצִי הַכֶּבֶשׁ וּלְמַטָּה בַמַּעֲרָב.** כֹּהֲנִים הַזּוֹכִים בַּפַּיִס בְּאֶבְרֵי הַתָּמִיד לְהַעֲלוֹתָם, לֹא הָיוּ מַעֲלִים אוֹתָם בְּפַעַם אַחַת, אֶלָּא נוֹתְנִים אוֹתָן עַל הַכֶּבֶשׁ, וְהוֹלְכִים לְלִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית לִקְרֹא אֶת שְׁמַע, וְאַחַר כָּךְ חוֹזְרִים וּמַקְרִיבִים הָאֵבָרִים עַל גַּבֵּי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ. וְקָתָנֵי הָכָא דְּנִתָּנִים מֵחֲצִי הַכֶּבֶשׁ וּלְמַטָּה, שֶׁהַכֶּבֶשׁ אָרֹךְ שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם אַמָּה, וּמֵחֶצְיוֹ וּלְמַטָּה נוֹתְנִים אוֹתָן. וְרֹחַב הַכֶּבֶשׁ שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה, וּבְחֶצְיוֹ שֶׁלְּצַד מַעֲרָב הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים אוֹתָן:


מחצי כבש ולמטה במערב – the Kohanim/priests who obtained the privilege by the arbitration regarding the limbs of the daily offering to bring them up would not bring them up all at one time, but would place them on the ramp and walk to the chamber of Hewn Stones (where the Sanhedrin sat) to recite the Shema and afterwards return and offer up the limbs on top of the Altar as it is taught [in our Mishnah] here, that they are placed on the lower half of he Altar-slope. For the ramp was thirty-two cubits long and from its half-way point downward, they would place them, and the width of ramp was sixteen cubits and on its lower half to the western side, they would place them (i.e., the limbs of the daily offering).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:2)


**וְשֶׁל רָאשֵׁי חֳדָשִׁים.** מֵחֶצְיוֹ וּלְמַעְלָה, דְּהַיְנוּ עַל כַּרְכֹּב הַמִּזְבֵּחַ שֶׁהוּא הַסּוֹבֵב. וּכְלַפֵּי הַמִּזְרָח:


ושל ראשי חדשים – from it half-way point and upwards which is on the rim of the Altar that surrounds it and toward the east.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:3)


**אֵינָן נוֹהֲגִים אֶלָּא בִפְנֵי הַבַּיִת.** שְׁקָלִים, מִשּׁוּם דְּצֹרֶךְ קָרְבָּן נִינְהוּ וְכֵיוָן שֶׁאֵין קָרְבָּן אֵין שְׁקָלִים. וּבִכּוּרִים, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כג) רֵאשִׁית בִּכּוּרֵי אַדְמָתְךָ תָּבִיא בֵּית ה'‏ אֱלֹהֶיךָ, בִּזְמַן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לְךָ בַּיִת יֵשׁ לְךָ בִּכּוּרִים, אֵין לְךָ בַּיִת אֵין לְךָ בִּכּוּרִים:


אינן נוהגים אלא בפני הבית – Shekalim because they are for the needs of a sacrifice, and since there is no sacrifice, there are no Shekalim. But regarding the First Fruits, as it is written (Exodus 23:19): “The choice first fruits of your soil you shall bring to the house of the LORD your God." At the time when you have a Temple, you have First Fruits; when you don’t have a Temple, you don’t have First Fruits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:4)


**אֲבָל מַעֲשַׂר דָּגָן וְכוּ'.** נוֹהֲגִים בִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת. וְשֶׁלֹּא בִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת. דִּקְדוּשַׁת הָאָרֶץ לֹא בָּטְלָה, הִלְכָּךְ צָרִיךְ לְהַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת. וּמַעֲשַׂר בְּהֵמָה, צָרִיךְ לְהַפְרִישׁ הָעֲשִׂירִי. וּבְמַסֶּכֶת בְּכוֹרוֹת אָמְרוּ שֶׁבִּטְּלוּ מַעֲשַׂר בְּהֵמָה דְּלֹא לֵיתוֹ בָּהּ לִידֵי תַּקָּלָה:


אבל מעשר דגן וכו' נוהגים בפני הבית ולשא בפני הבית – for the Holiness of the Land [of Israel] is never invalidated. Therefore, it is necessary to separate out Terumot and Tithes and for the Tithe of Cattle, you must separate a tenth. But in Tractate Bekhorot (see Chapter 9, Mishnah 1), they stated that they invalidated the tithe of the cattle so that it should not come regarding it as a stumbling block/snare.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:5)


**הַמַּקְדִּישׁ שְׁקָלִים וּבִכּוּרִים.** דְּאֵין נוֹהֲגִים אֶלָּא בִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת, אִם הִפְרִישָׁן שֶׁלֹּא בִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת קַדְשֵׁי:


המקדיש שקלים ובכורים – which was not practiced other than when the Temple stood, but if one separated them when the Temple no long existed, they are sanctified.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shekalim 8:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shekalim/r/8:8:6)


**בִּכּוּרִים קֹדֶשׁ אֵינָן קֹדֶשׁ.** כֵּיוָן דִּבְהֶדְיָא כְּתִיב בְּהוּ (שמות כג) תָּבִיא בֵּית ה'‏ אֱלֹהֶיךָ, אֲפִלּוּ הִפְרִישָׁן בְּדִיעֲבַד לֹא קַדְשֵׁי, וְאֵין שֵׁם בִּכּוּרִים חָל עֲלֵיהֶן שֶׁלֹּא בִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, הַמַּקְדִּישׁ לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת שְׁקָלִים וּבִכּוּרִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ כְּבָר. וּלְפֵרוּשׁוֹ לֹא אִתְבָּרֵיר טַעֲמָא דְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אַמַּאי שְׁקָלִים קַדְשֵׁי וּבִכּוּרִים לֹא קַדְשֵׁי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


בכורים אינן קדש – since it is explicitly written concerning them (Exodus 23:19): “you shall bring to the House of the LORD your God,” even if he had separated them post-facto, they are not holy and the name/title of Bikkurim/First Fruits does not take effect upon them because the Temple doesn’t exist. But Maimonides explains that one who sanctifies Shekalim and First Fruits for the upkeep and maintenance of the Temple that he already has, but according to his explanation, the reason for why, according to Rabbi Shimon that Shekalim are holy, and First Fruits is not holy is not clear. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.
